We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [149] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究LLM在教育评估任务中是否受学生人口统计特征影响，发现显式与隐式人口信号均会导致模型改变评分、反馈和回答，且产生不可预测的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于学生评估，但学生人口统计特征如何影响其表现尚不清楚。考虑人口特征有时必要（如为低教育水平用户改善可读性），但也可能引发歧视（如给低社会经济背景学生打低分）。

Method: 设计受控提示，测试①显式人口效应（直接提及人口细节）与②隐式效应（用对话历史作为人口信号）。在三个任务（自动作文评分、形成性反馈、元语言问答）上测试六个先进LLM。

Result: 显式和隐式条件下，模型都会捕捉人口线索并相应改变评分、反馈和回答。当明确提及教育水平时，LLM频繁调整反馈可读性；隐式条件产生不可预测的偏见，如问答中低教育水平回答获得更低情感分数。

Conclusion: 结果清楚表明LLM在教育评估任务中存在人口统计敏感性，需警惕其带来的歧视与不可预测偏见。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [150] [PaperDoctor: Evidence-Grounded and Actionable Feedback for Scientific Papers in Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16995)
*Kevin Qinghong Lin,Siyuan Hu,Pan Lu,Yu Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Owen Queen,Yupeng Chen,Jialin Yu,Junchi Yu,Zifeng Ding,Yuanfeng Ji,Sheng Liu,Jindong Gu,Linjie Li,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PaperDoctor，一个用于论文投稿前反馈的智能体框架，将自动评估从"裁判"转变为"诊断者"，通过分层验证与可复现实验重跑，生成可审计、可操作的评语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科研智能体加速研究产出的同时，也可能让有缺陷的论断大规模进入文献；人类导师虽能通过细致、可追溯的反馈发现问题，但人工评估成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 三大创新：(1) 分层评估框架——L1 表层筛查、L2 类型化验证器将论断路由到对应证据、L3 复现器按优先级重跑实验，覆盖写作、排版、参考文献、代码、理论、前人工作与实验；(2) 每项发现包含观察、具体证据定位（句子/公式/代码行）与修改建议，使批评可审计、可执行；(3) 依据论断重要性与算力预算，选择性重建并重跑实验，揭示仅凭稿件不可见的可复现性缺口。

Result: 在 30 篇在投论文上取得 70.6% 的一致率及全部正向整体评分；在 40 篇涵盖机器学习、自然科学与社会科学、包含人类与 AI 撰写且有代码的稿件上验证。相比人类与其他智能体评审，反馈更可审计、批评与建议天然配对，并补充了人类评审常忽视的维度。另开发了可让作者浏览基于其论文的发现的交互界面。

Conclusion: PaperDoctor 将自动论文评估重构为"诊断而非裁决"，是迈向更严谨的 AI 辅助科学发现中"AI 导师"的具体一步。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents are reshaping the research ecosystem, but they can also let flawed claims enter the literature at scale. Human advisors catch such issues in drafts through careful, traceable feedback, yet advisor-style assessment requires extensive manual effort and does not scale. To shift automated paper assessment from a judge to a diagnostician, we introduce PaperDoctor, an agent framework for pre-submission feedback with three key innovations. First, a holistic hierarchical framework evaluates writing, layout, references, code, theory, prior work, and experiments through three layers: L1 surface screening, L2 typed verifiers that route each claim to the appropriate evidence, and L3 reproducers that rerun experiments by priority. Second, each finding contains an observation, a pointer to specific evidence such as a sentence, equation, or code line, and a revision suggestion, making critiques auditable and actionable. Third, PaperDoctor selectively rebuilds and reruns experiments based on claim importance and compute budget, surfacing reproducibility gaps and quantitative limitations that are invisible from the manuscript alone. We evaluate PaperDoctor on 30 in-progress papers, yielding 70.6% agreement and all positive holistic scores, and on 40 manuscripts across machine learning, natural science, and social science, covering human- and AI-authored papers with code. Overall, PaperDoctor produces more auditable feedback than human and other agentic reviewers, pairs critiques with concrete suggestions by design, and complements dimensions often overlooked by human reviewers. We also develop an interactive interface that lets authors browse findings grounded in their paper. PaperDoctor reframes automated paper assessment as diagnosis rather than verdict, taking a concrete step toward AI advisors for more rigorous AI-assisted scientific discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UNRESTSENT200K，一个约20万条孟加拉语危机情感数据集，涵盖2024年7-8月孟加拉国起义的五个事件阶段，并通过人类标注与多种模型基准测试揭示上下文与时间变化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的危机情感分析极具挑战，因为语言、语境与公众反应在危机中快速变化，缺少相应数据集与基准。

Method: 构建UNRESTSENT200K数据集：采集Facebook与YouTube约200K条评论，覆盖五个事件对齐阶段（从升级、断网到政权过渡与后续洪灾危机）；每条评论关联父帖以支持有无话语语境的评估；由14名孟加拉语母语标注者全人工标注并经资深人员验证；基准测试微调编码器、提示LLM与LoRA微调LLM。

Result: 标注一致性较高（kappa=0.73，alpha=0.71），盲审一致率94.2%；父帖上下文能持续提升性能；跨阶段时间漂移导致性能大幅下降；强LLM表现良好但在讽刺、隐晦政治指涉及阶段依赖语义上仍有困难。

Conclusion: UNRESTSENT200K为低资源危机话语中上下文感知与时间鲁棒的情感分析研究提供了基准，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at https://sami0055.github.io/UNRESTSENT200K/

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多跳问答的失败分为检索失败（未检索到所需段落）和抽取失败（已检索到段落但无法抽取所需事实，称为事实落地缺口），抽取失败约占各跳缺陷的近一半，且检索类改进无法解决，揭示了两种需要不同解决方案的根本瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳问答系统虽能力增强，但失败通常被归因于"未找到正确文档"。作者质疑这一归因在单步推理层面是否成立，因为此前少有研究检验。

Method: 在三个标准多跳QA基准上对逐跳推理缺陷进行分解分析，区分为检索失败与抽取失败，并测试多种检索干预手段能否缓解抽取失败。

Result: 抽取失败占各跳缺陷的近一半，且对标准检索指标不可见；所有测试的检索干预均无法解决，确立仅靠检索改进的天花板。该缺口严重程度因基准和问题类型而异，但在每个数据集上都存在。

Conclusion: 检索失败与抽取失败是本质不同的瓶颈，需要不同的解决方案，这一区分在当前评测实践中缺失，呼吁重新设计多跳QA的评估与改进方向。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [153] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviScope是成对反事实基准，固定问题并增删/干扰/矛盾证据，用配对指标揭示答案准确率掩盖的模型接地行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接地语言模型系统只用最终答案准确率评估，但正确答案可能是无依据的、来源错误的，或在证据不足/矛盾时仍被给出，因此需要区分这类失败模式。

Method: EviScope 是成对反事实基准：固定问题，对证据做添加、删除、干扰、矛盾四种条件操作，v1.1含40个四条件四元组，修好反事实声明并提供跨度级支持标注；用Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash共960次金标盲生成，采用配对指标评估。

Result: 配对指标暴露了准确率隐藏的模型相关接地行为。两个本地开源模型上，显式证据-动作门控在QCS上不如vanilla RAG：Qwen为0.15对0.50，Llama为0.10对0.375。Gemini在两种提示下联合成功率达0.944，但在插入矛盾后仍对5%冲突案例作答。

Conclusion: EviScope能区分无依据作答、冲突盲视和错误的非作答动作，而不是仅给答案打分。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [154] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过"反事实自解释"研究LLM自解释的忠实性，发现模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能轻松生成自解释，但这些解释未必忠实于模型真实行为，需要实证检验。

Method: 采用反事实自解释：让模型最小化编辑输入以改变自身预测。在情感分析和自然语言推理任务上，评估LLaMA-3与Qwen-2.5家族的十个指令微调模型，测量忠实性、最小性和与人类标注理由的对齐度。

Result: 模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素：更大模型更可能生成翻转自身预测、且针对决策相关证据的反事实。理由引导条件产生编辑最小且更贴合人类的反事实，但并未一致提升忠实性。

Conclusion: 反事实自解释可提供关于模型决策的有用行为证据，但其可靠性强烈依赖模型能力，应经实证验证而非假设成立。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [155] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析跨越一个多世纪的数百万首歌词，发现越战、9/11和COVID-19等文化剧变会在艺术家个体与群体层面改变其语言行为（自我指涉减少、认知加工词汇增多）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化剧变影响社会生活诸多方面，已有研究探讨其对语言模式的影响，但少有研究在大规模流行媒体中隔离剧变对个体的影响，尤其是艺术家内部与艺术家之间的信号。

Method: 分析跨越一个多世纪、数百万首歌词，考察越战、9/11恐怖袭击和COVID-19相关的困扰信号，采用五年基线进行对比，区分艺术家内部与艺术家之间的变化。

Result: 相比五年基线，越战和9/11后自我指涉（心理疏离的标志）显著减少；剧变后认知加工词汇较剧变前升高，表明艺术家更努力从巨大 disruption 中建构意义；内容上，剧变后更多书写"生活与自由"（社会状况），更少书写"求偶与夜生活"（人际联系）。

Conclusion: 文化剧变改变个体与集体的言语行为，证明其对社会具有深远影响。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [156] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出阿拉伯语隐喻标注指南AraMIP，基于MIPVU框架并适配阿拉伯语特性，区分isti'ara、kinaya、tashbih三类比喻语言，并标注了300句试点数据集，为阿拉伯语比喻语言研究奠定资源基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻研究与语言创造力、语言使用和认知过程密切相关，受到越来越多关注。英语等语言的隐喻识别与标注已有大量工作，但阿拉伯语在这一领域资源匮乏，缺乏标准化标注指南和资源。

Method: 提出阿拉伯隐喻识别程序（AraMIP），在广泛使用的MIPVU框架基础上进行适配，考虑阿拉伯语的语言特性。将阿拉伯语比喻语言分为三类：isti'ara（隐喻）、kinaya（转喻/间接表达）、tashbih（明喻），并对300句（5277词）的试点数据集进行标注。

Result: 标注与分析揭示了阿拉伯语特有的关键挑战，包括形态复杂性、词典义项排序不一致，以及缺乏面向标注者的标准化语境材料。

Conclusion: 该工作迈出了构建标准化阿拉伯语比喻实例的第一步，有助于开发更大规模的标注资源，从而支持未来阿拉伯语比喻语言研究。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [157] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO是一个分层双环框架，利用LLM层间功能不对称性解决无草稿模型投机解码中的陈旧候选和高验证成本问题，实现2.4×-2.9×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无草稿模型的投机解码虽有前景，但受限于陈旧的草稿候选以及高昂的验证计算成本。

Method: 提出ECHO分层双环框架，利用LLM层间功能不对称性：早期层具高判别效率，最终层具权威分布。将推理分为高频内环和低频外环。内环用早期层bonus logits进行低成本快速多步草稿树探索；外环通过状态复用机制进行权威全模型验证，并利用最终层bonus logits修正现有路径、补充高置信候选供后续循环使用。

Result: 在多基准测试上显著提升平均接受token数，实现2.4×-2.9×加速，超越现有SOTA基线，工程开销极小且无需额外部署参数。

Conclusion: ECHO有效提升LLM推理效率，性能优于现有方法，但最优加速效果依赖一次性微调。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/whucs21Mzy/ECHO.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在6层解码器中插入轻量级持久循环记忆模块（跨注意力观测+GRU更新+门控融合），仅增3.7%参数，验证损失降28.5%，显著减少过拟合，效果源于持久记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索用轻量循环记忆桥接Transformer层，以改善小规模语言模型的泛化能力并减少过拟合，且验证收益来源是拓扑结构而非辅助目标。

Method: 对decoder-only Transformer做架构改动：持久循环状态通过跨注意力观测隐藏表示，经GRU更新，再以门控加法调制后续处理；该模块置于6层Transformer的下半与上半之间。并进行受控消融（相同拓扑无辅助损失、随机辅助损失）及表示探测。

Result: 仅增3.7%参数，评估损失从2.438±0.004降至1.743±0.018（28.5%降幅），5个随机种子下p<0.01显著；泛化差距0.12 vs 0.26；相同拓扑无辅助损失效果等价，随机辅助损失无收益；持久状态可编码叙事位置（52% vs 33%随机水平）。

Conclusion: 以轻量循环记忆桥接Transformer层是提升小规模语言模型泛化的简单有效方法；改进完全来自持久记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"设计幻觉"(design illusions)概念，指持续依赖已被推翻的假设而导致的错误信心，并给出一个识别、分类、刻画和利用幻觉的概念化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在开放环境中运行，无法预知所有情况，利益相关者依赖假设并将其形式化为设计模型；运行时现实可能推翻这些假设，造成"现实差距"。现有方法多致力于让仿真更贴近现实，缺乏统一方法论来结构化利用被推翻的假设作为可复用设计知识。

Method: 提出概念化流程(conceptual pipeline)，用于幻觉感知(illusions-awareness)：识别、分类、刻画并利用设计幻觉，将其转化为可操作的设计知识。

Result: 论文为概念性工作，主要贡献是提出设计幻觉概念及对应的幻觉感知流程（摘要未给出实证结果）。

Conclusion: 被推翻的假设应被显式化、结构化并作为知识加以利用，以支持更好的设计决策，从而应对CPS开放环境中的现实差距问题。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究LLM在零样本下识别社交媒体隐藏叙事的能力，发现人类撰写的叙事描述可显著提升性能，而自动描述和少样本因框架偏移常降低准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM能否凭借丰富上下文知识在更深层的语用层面解读信息，超越基础情感或主题分析，从而零样本地识别隐藏叙事。

Method: 在Dipromats和SemEval数据集上开展实验，对比人类撰写描述、自动生成描述、少样本示例三种设置，并测试集成方法（如多数投票）及模型规模、提示变化敏感性的影响。

Result: 人类撰写描述显著提升性能且无需训练样本；自动描述或少样本常因框架微妙偏移而降低准确率；多数投票等集成方法增强鲁棒性；更大模型表现最佳且对提示变化更不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM可在零样本下有效检测战略性叙事，结合简单集成与人类撰写描述可媲美监督系统，为缺乏训练数据的众多领域提供可扩展的叙事检测方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASURRE基准数据集，用于检测LLM辅助的在线问卷作答，发现persona-grounded智能体难以检测，但存在可聚合的行为痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被用于完成在线问卷，威胁问卷研究的有效性，但如何检测此类使用尚缺乏研究。

Method: 构建ASURRE数据集，覆盖从全文生成、修改到persona-grounded智能体作答的多种策略；用多个LLM在三份真实跨学科问卷上生成作答，并与真人作答配对；评估现有MGT检测器，并提出一种无需训练的小样本聚合器整合行为线索。

Result: 朴素AI作答易被检测，但模拟完整受访者的persona智能体使检测器接近随机水平；智能体作答无法完全复现受访者行为并留下独特痕迹；聚合器在智能体场景下比最佳现有检测器平均AUROC提升+0.14。

Conclusion: 现有MGT检测器面对智能体式问卷作答效果有限，而聚合行为线索是有效的检测方向。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at https://github.com/mike-qz-wang/ASURRE.

</details>


### [162] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍SHROOM-Visions共享任务的提交方案，使用多个微调视觉语言模型作为独立标注者，通过字符级多数投票融合跨度预测，并探索激活探针；在四种语言中的三种排名第一，所有语言和指标均登上领奖台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务目标是检测并分类视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉字符跨度，跨四种语言，需要可靠的标注与判定方法。

Method: 微调多个视觉语言模型作为独立标注者，对跨度预测进行字符级多数投票融合，并额外探索激活探针方法。

Result: 在四种语言中的三种排名第一，在所有语言和所有指标上均进入前三名（登上领奖台）。

Conclusion: 模型间的不一致性与人类标注者之间的不一致性具有一致性，说明多样化模型集成可作为可靠的标注信号。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [163] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于表示（KV-cache）和基于参数（微调）的知识注入方法进行了受控对比，发现Cartridges（KV）在多数场景下最优，但存在灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要获取预训练数据之外的新知识，可通过上下文窗口文档、编码进模型参数或注入潜在表示实现，但各方法在效率、成本和性能上各有取舍，缺乏统一比较。本文旨在进行受控对比。

Method: 在五个知识密集型基准上，对基于表示（KV-cache）和基于参数（微调）的适应方法进行受控对比，涵盖oracle设置和多文档检索场景，比较Cartridges、Compaction及参数化方法。

Result: Oracle设置下，Cartridges（KV）在几乎所有存储预算下最准确，比参数化方法高10分；Compaction仅在低压缩率下匹配Cartridges，压缩率超50倍时落后参数化方法10分。多文档检索场景下，Cartridges是唯一匹配ICL的方法，领先参数化方法29分、Compaction 15分。但Cartridges与全量微调及大MLP适配器一样存在灾难性遗忘，控制基准下降6%，编码任务下降13%。

Conclusion: 表示-based的Cartridges在准确性和检索场景中表现最佳，但代价是灾难性遗忘；不同知识注入方法各有取舍，需根据场景权衡。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [164] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ECHO，一个面向中文全双工对话的成对诊断基准，用于区分需要让出话轮的打断与允许继续说话的反向反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常独立评估事件，可能奖励固定的动作偏好，而非上下文敏感的决策，无法真实反映全双工对话系统的轮流可靠性。

Method: ECHO将相同重叠转录但对比性多轮前文上下文的例子成对呈现，一个要求Yield，另一个要求Keep；并加入off-talk例子以诊断不必要的让出。提出pair accuracy指标，要求成对两个成员都判断正确。

Result: 在多个全双工系统上的实验显示，大多数系统明显偏向Yield，在打断上表现远好于反向反馈，而另一个系统相对平衡。

Conclusion: 仅评估打断会高估实际轮流可靠性；ECHO及其元数据将公开发布。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA等LLM，并结合MoA技术实现医疗文本的通俗语言适配（PLA），通过提示策略对比与QLoRA微调，验证了其在保持核心内容前提下提升医疗信息可读性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗文本专业性强，患者难以理解，自动化通俗语言适配（PLA）可在技术性医疗语言与患者阅读能力之间架起桥梁，弥合理解鸿沟。

Method: 采用GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA等大模型进行文本简化；对比不同提示策略；用QLoRA对不同LLM进行微调；并引入Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）技术提升适应性与鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明LLM驱动的PLA具有有效性，能够在不丢失关键信息的同时提升医疗信息的可理解性；提示策略对比、QLoRA微调与MoA集成都取得了积极效果。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的PLA在让医疗信息更易理解且保留核心内容方面具有潜力，零样本/少样本场景下尤为适用。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [166] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MéTRON-FR 是一个在 9247 万法语词上预训练的 125M GPT-2，提交至 BabyLM 2026 Strict 赛道，并考察其跨语言迁移与对齐特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估儿童规模（child-scale）法语模型的语法能力，并探究跨语言任务中单 token 评分指标是否可靠。

Method: 在法语语料上预训练 125M GPT-2；采用跨语言 GLUE 协议（法语任务数据翻译 + rank-16 LoRA）；用双语词典归纳做嵌入对齐；并进行消融研究。

Result: QFrBLiMP 达 85.97±0.17%，加权排行榜 62.80%；任务呈现梯度——关系型任务提升、世界知识型任务退化；BLI p@1=68.84±8.61%，为随机的 18 倍。

Conclusion: 跨语言对齐反映语法能力而非训练时长；单 token 零样本评分受分词器和模板伪影主导，建议将分词器替换敏感性、安慰剂对照提示及母语最小对比基准作为标准诊断手段。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [167] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究剪枝对上下文接地工具调用的影响，发现稠密模型安全剪枝区间窄且退化陡峭，MoE容忍度更高，剪枝先损伤接地特异性再损伤schema级意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝能降低LLM部署成本，但其对上下文接地工具调用能力的影响尚不明确。

Method: 对4个LLM（涵盖稠密Transformer、稠密混合及MoE架构）施加深度、宽度、混合及专家剪枝，经剪枝后SFT，在3个智能家居数据集上评估超19,500个实例，并从动作组件（操作、设备、参数、取值）与任务复杂度两个维度刻画退化。

Result: 稠密模型安全剪枝区间窄、退化陡峭；MoE模型可容忍更多剪枝；剪枝先损伤接地特异性，后损伤schema级意图；激进稠密剪枝会引发系统性过度拒答。

Conclusion: 为可靠工具执行选择剪枝模型时，需超越聚合准确率进行评估。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning. Pruning degrades grounded specificity before schema-level intent, and aggressive dense pruning can induce systematic over-refusal. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating pruning beyond aggregate accuracy when selecting pruned LLMs for reliable tool execution.

</details>


### [168] [When Should LLMs Abstain? Chain-of-Self-Questioning for Selective Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17516)
*Ali Şenol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ) 提示框架，让模型在回答前先评估信息是否充分，从而在信息不足时选择弃答，减少无依据的错误作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在缺乏事实依据时仍会生成流畅答案，导致错误承诺（wrong commitment）。当无依据作答的代价高于转介或人工复核时，需要一种可显式、可调的回答或弃答决策机制。

Method: 提出纯提示框架 CoSQ：在答案承诺前显式评估回答问题所需的信息。在 TruthfulQA 多选验证集（817 题）上，用 11 个开源与托管模型家族、17 种条件评测三种 CoSQ 变体，并采用最终平衡选项协议（如 Grounded-CoSQ 阈值 τ=0.90），另在 Natural Questions 短答任务上做次级验证。

Result: 在平衡选项协议下，Grounded-CoSQ（τ=0.90）将平均无条件错误承诺率从 CoT 的 13.1% 降至 8.9%（相对下降 32.1%），同时将已作答准确率从 86.9% 提升至 89.7%，并回答了 87.6% 的问题。改进在全部 11 个模型和所有评估阈值上均成立。Critical-CoSQ 与 Adaptive-CoSQ 覆盖率分别为 88.6% 和 86.5%，且均比基线更可靠。NQ 短答结果提供了一致的开放式证据。

Conclusion: 自我评估可支持显式、可调的回答或弃答决策，在无依据承诺代价高于转介或复核的场景中有效提升可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers when their factual support is weak. This paper introduces Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ), a prompt-only framework that makes answer commitment conditional on an explicit assessment of the information required to answer a question. We evaluate three CoSQ variants under seventeen conditions on the 817-item TruthfulQA multiple-choice validation set using eleven open-weight and hosted model families. In the final balanced-option protocol, Grounded-CoSQ at τ=0.90 reduces the mean unconditional wrong-commitment rate from 13.1% under chain-of-thought prompting to 8.9%, a 32.1% relative reduction, while increasing answered accuracy from 86.9% to 89.7% and answering 87.6% of questions. Both improvements hold for all eleven models and at every evaluated threshold. Critical-CoSQ and Adaptive-CoSQ provide neighboring operating points with 88.6% and 86.5% coverage, respectively, while remaining more reliable than the baseline. A secondary Natural Questions Short-Answer evaluation provides convergent open-form evidence. These findings show that self-assessment can support explicit, tunable answer-or-abstain decisions when an unsupported commitment is more costly than referral or review.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Fast-Convergent Meta-RL via Gradient-Clustered BS Sampling for Edge Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16370)
*Farnaz Niknia,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出面向独立BS无线边缘缓存网络的元强化学习框架，用梯度聚类采样降低MAML元梯度方差，加速元训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各BS请求率与内容流行度不同但决策问题本质相同，逐个从零训练RL代理冗余缓慢；元RL可学习共享初始化快速适应，但大规模下元训练成为瓶颈——每轮元迭代均匀随机抽样BS导致元梯度估计高方差，现有元RL缓存框架未解决此问题。

Method: 每个BS运行本地PPO代理，将缓存问题建模为基于内容流行度、大小、生命周期和重要性的半马尔可夫决策过程(SMDP)；通过MAML式循环学习共享元策略；提出基于梯度的聚类，将BS按局部梯度相似性分组，每轮元迭代按簇大小比例从各簇抽样以扩展元训练并加速收敛。

Result: 通过ANOVA式梯度方差分解证明：在BS异质性下，该聚类采样策略的元梯度估计器方差严格低于均匀随机采样。

Conclusion: 梯度聚类采样有效缓解元训练瓶颈，为跨独立异质BS的可扩展元强化学习缓存提供了高方差问题的理论与方法解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless edge caching networks typically consist of many independent Base Stations (BSs), each facing its own request rate and content popularity profile. Training a Reinforcement Learning (RL) caching agent from scratch at every BS forces each agent to relearn, through slow trial and error, a decision problem that is structurally identical across the network. Meta-reinforcement learning removes this redundancy by learning a shared initialization that adapts to any BS in a few local updates; however, meta-training itself becomes the bottleneck at scale: the meta-gradient must be estimated from a small subset of BSs at each meta-iteration, and sampling this subset uniformly at random yields a high-variance estimate, an issue existing meta-RL caching frameworks leave unaddressed. This paper proposes a meta-reinforcement learning framework for caching across independent, non-overlapping BSs that directly targets this bottleneck. Each BS runs a local Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent, formulated as a Semi-Markov Decision Process (SMDP) over content popularity, size, lifetime, and importance, while a shared meta-policy is learned via a Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML)-style loop. To scale meta-training and accelerate convergence, we introduce gradient-based clustering, which groups BSs by local gradient similarity and draws from every cluster, in proportion to its size, at each meta-iteration. We prove, via an Analysis of Variance (ANOVA)-style decomposition of gradient variance, that this strategy yields a strictly lower-variance meta-gradient estimator than uniform random sampling under BS heterogeneity.

</details>


### [170] [The Price of the Golden 6G Band: Evaluation of Beam Management Effort in FR3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16839)
*Clémence Altmeyerhenzien,Ljiljana Simić,Marina Petrova*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究FR3（7.125-24.25 GHz）城市网络中的波束管理开销，发现实现高稳定移动吞吐需大量波束管理，低频波束跟踪要求较低但非相邻波束切换率与FR2相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FR3被视为6G的"黄金频段"，传播特性优于FR2、带宽大于FR1。复用FR1基础设施部署FR3需gNB采用天线阵列并执行波束管理，而这在FR2已被证明具有挑战性，因此亟需刻画FR3的波束管理开销。

Method: 对FR3城市网络中的波束管理开销进行广泛研究与表征，指标包括：波束对齐敏感性、方向链路机会数量、gNB切换率与波束切换率、波束转向距离。

Result: 实现高且稳定的移动吞吐需要跨FR3频段的大量波束管理；低频段由于波束更宽，波束跟踪要求不如高频严格；但切换至非相邻波束的波束切换率在FR3与FR2相对相当。

Conclusion: FR3部署仍面临显著波束管理挑战，尤其体现在非相邻波束切换率上，与FR2相当，需在系统设计中予以重视。

Abstract: Frequency Range 3 (FR3), 7.125-24.25 GHz, regarded as the "golden band" for 6G networks, has less challenging propagation characteristics than FR2 while offering much wider bandwidth for high data rate applications than FR1. Reusing existing FR1 infrastructure for FR3 network deployments requires gNodeBs (gNBs) to employ antenna arrays and perform beam management, which has proven challenging at FR2. In this paper, we extensively study and characterize the beam management effort in an FR3 urban network, in terms of: beam alignment sensitivity, number of directional link opportunities, gNB handover and beam switch rates, and beam steering distance. Our results show that achieving a high and stable mobile throughput requires significant beam management effort across FR3 bands. While the beam tracking requirements are less stringent at the lower frequencies due to wider beams, the beam switching rate to a non-adjacent beam is relatively comparable at FR3 and FR2.

</details>


### [171] [A Mechanical Antenna for Improving Capacity Fairness in Dynamic Multi-Station Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16877)
*Akihito Taya,Yuuki Nishiyama,Kaoru Sezaki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出机械式Wi-Fi天线控制系统，通过黑盒优化器与容量变化检测自适应调整3D天线朝向，提升动态多站点场景的信道容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AP采用静态天线部署，固定朝向在动态传播环境中往往次优，难以应对室内IoT多设备高带宽数据流的采集需求。

Method: 机械驱动物理天线，结合针对状态的黑盒优化器与基于容量的环境变化检测，自主响应所感知的无线环境来调整3D天线朝向。

Result: 在动态站点组合下提升信道容量；在瞬态遮挡时避免不必要的重新优化；在持续遮挡、设备迁移等持续环境变化后触发重新优化。

Conclusion: 机械式自适应天线朝向可有效克服静态部署的局限，兼顾性能提升与优化开销控制。

Abstract: While indoor Internet of Things (IoT) and sensor networks increasingly rely on Wi-Fi access points (APs) to collect high-bandwidth data streams from multiple devices, conventional APs rely on static antenna deployments, whose fixed orientations are often suboptimal in dynamic propagation environments. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a mechanical Wi-Fi antenna control system that adaptively optimizes its 3D antenna orientation for dynamic multi-station scenarios. The proposed system autonomously actuates its physical antennas in response to perceived radio environments by combining state-specific black-box optimizers and capacity-based environment change detection. The evaluation results show that the proposed system improves channel capacity under dynamic station combinations, avoids unnecessary re-optimization under transient blockages, and triggers re-optimization after sustained environmental changes such as continuous blockage and device relocation.

</details>


### [172] [AeroLat: Channel-Aware Latent Space Semantic Communication for Decentralized UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16947)
*Rajdeep Ghosh,Goparaju Venkata Seshachala Sree Vatsava,Sudip Misra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出 AeroLat，一种信道感知的潜在语义通信框架，通过证据注入缓解同质冻结模型在离散感知输入下的状态坍缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带宽受限、时变的无线链路下，UAV 群采用潜在空间通信是一种有吸引力的替代方案。但同质冻结模型在离散感知输入提示下，其广播状态会向共享提示模板坍缩，影响通信多样性。

Method: AeroLat 使用证据注入构建信道感知的潜在语义通信框架；潜在状态经由显式通信模型处理，包含带宽受限序列化、加性噪声和信息陈旧，从而联合评估通信保真度与群体协同。

Result: 多次种子仿真中，AeroLat 对编解码器选择、故障和群体规模增长保持鲁棒，稳定复现潜在群体异常，而消融（no-whitening）对照组恢复坍缩。误相似度降低 97.5%。

Conclusion: AeroLat 能有效缓解潜在状态坍缩，提升潜在语义通信的可靠性与协同表现，适用于去中心化 UAV 集群通信。

Abstract: Communication in latent space offers an intriguing alternative to symbolic messages for decentralized autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms operating over bandwidth-constrained, time-varying wireless links. However, when homogeneous frozen models are prompted with discretized perceptual inputs, their broadcast states collapse toward the shared prompt template. In view of this, we propose AeroLat, a channel-aware latent semantic communication framework that uses evidence injection. The resulting latent states are then passed through an explicit communication model that encompasses bandwidth-limited serialization, additive noise and information staleness, which facilitates a joint assessment of communication fidelity and swarm-level coordination. Across multi-seed simulations, AeroLat provably remains resilient to codec choice, faults and increasing swarm size. It consistently reproduces the latent-swarm anomaly, while no-whitening controls recover the collapse. In particular, AeroLat is capable of reducing false similarity by 97.5%.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond Measurement Metrics: A Human-Centered Framework for Semantic Validation of Network Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17014)
*Igor Cherepanov,David Sessler,Alex Ulmer,Thorsten May,Jörn Kohlhammer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个适配网络流量分类的知识生成框架，结合数据、ML模型、可解释性、可视化和专家推理，弥补仅评估预测性能的不足，支持语义层面的模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML在网络流量分类中预测性能很高，但高预测性能不代表模型学到的是语义上有意义、可信的模式，可能依赖虚假相关。传统评估只看预测性能，无法判断模型是否依赖有意义的模式。

Method: 将知识生成框架适配到网络流量分类，融合数据、ML模型、可解释性（XAI）、可视化和专家推理，支持对模型行为和数据预处理的迭代探索、验证与改进。框架依据来自文献、基准数据集分析、XAI流量分类实践经验及专家反馈。

Result: 该框架为语义模型验证提供实践指导，在预测性能之外补充了语义验证和人类专业知识。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于开发不仅准确、而且鲁棒且可信的网络流量分类模型。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become the dominant approach for network traffic classification, achieving very high predictive performance. However, a model is only valuable if it learns semantically meaningful and trustworthy patterns rather than exploiting spurious correlations. Conventional evaluation practices predominantly assess predictive performance. Consequently, whether the model relies on semantically meaningful patterns remains unknown. To address these challenges, we adapt the knowledge generation framework for network traffic classification. The adapted framework combines data, ML models, explainability, visualization, and expert reasoning to support the iterative exploration, verification, and refinement of model behavior and data preprocessing. The framework is grounded in findings from the literature, benchmark dataset analyses, practical experience with XAI-based traffic classification, and expert feedback, providing practical guidance for semantic model validation. By complementing predictive performance with semantic validation and human expertise, the proposed framework supports the development of network traffic classification models that are not only accurate but also robust and trustworthy.

</details>


### [174] [Agentic RDZ: Autonomous Zone Management with AI Agents and an FR3 Coexistence Use Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17110)
*Minh Dat Nguyen,Gabriele Gemmi,Tamerlan Aghayev,Paolo Testolina,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出首个用LLM智能体实现无线电动态区(RDZ)的A-RDZ架构，在FR3 O-RAN测试台上验证了自动检测干扰违规并修复保护配额的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RDZ原型依赖手工规则和预定义流程进行自动化，当实验遇到设计时未预料的硬件损伤、用户流程、设备或干扰机制时变得脆弱，无法灵活应对。

Method: 基于GENESIS智能体框架构建A-RDZ架构，让智能体用LLM执行频谱管理、实验管理、策略解释和区域编排，将自主推理与确定性策略门和近实时反射机制结合，确保智能体提升效果但不削弱保护保证。在硬件在环FR3（7.125–24.25 GHz）O-RAN测试台上验证：5G NR实验与仿真的FSS地球站共存。

Result: 端到端用例中，监控智能体从实时频谱证据中检测到发射违规，编排器选择既能恢复干扰预算又保持实验运行的缓解措施，并通过O-RAN控制平面应用和验证。报告了检测到缓解的延迟分解。

Conclusion: 论证了智能体化RDZ的可行性，同时讨论了智能体运行的实际限制，包括非确定性推理和决策到行动转换等问题。

Abstract: Radio Dynamic Zones (RDZs) allow wireless experiments to operate outside conventional spectrum regulations while continuously guaranteeing protection for incumbent users. Existing RDZ prototypes automate this task procedurally, through handcrafted rules and predefined workflows, and become brittle when experiments encounter hardware impairments, user workflows and devices, or interference mechanisms not anticipated at design time. This paper introduces the agentic RDZ (A-RDZ), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first RDZ realization in which agents use Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform spectrum management, experiment management, policy interpretation, and zone orchestration. Built on the GENESIS agentic framework, the architecture pairs autonomous reasoning with a deterministic policy gate and near-real-time (near-RT) reflexes, so that agents can improve outcomes but never weaken the zone's protection guarantee. We validate the A-RDZ on a hardware-in-the-loop Frequency Range 3 (FR3) (7.125-24.25 GHz) Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) testbed in which a 5G New Radio (NR) experiment coexists with an emulated Fixed Satellite Service (FSS) earth-station incumbent. In an end-to-end use case, the monitoring agent detects an emission violation from live spectrum evidence, the orchestrator selects a mitigation that restores the interference budget while keeping the experiment running, and the action is applied and verified through the O-RAN control plane. We report the detection-to-mitigation latency decomposition and discuss the practical limits of agentic operation, including non-deterministic reasoning and decision-to-action translation.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Automated Comment Moderation Enhances Social Media Advertising Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16005)
*Jiwoon Park,Julian De Freitas*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过六项研究（含两项大规模田野实验）证明，AI自动审核并隐藏有害评论能因果性地提升广告效果（转化率、广告支出回报率、购买意愿），但收益取决于平台是否透明披露品牌的审核行为及被审核评论的类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体广告让品牌同时暴露于潜在客户与未经筛选的用户评论中。评论虽能增强真实感与互动，但也带来垃圾信息、仇恨言论和负面用户生成内容等声誉风险。尽管AI评论审核方案日益普及，却缺乏因果证据表明"隐藏有害评论"能否提升广告效果。

Method: 开展六项实证研究，包括两项大规模田野实验与四项在线实验，检验自动化有害评论审核对广告表现的因果影响，并考察平台透明度与评论类型等治理层面的边界条件。

Result: 自动审核有害评论能因果性地提升广告表现，包括转化率、广告支出回报率与购买意愿。审核收益依赖于平台是否透明披露品牌的审核行为，以及被审核评论的类型。当品牌透明披露自身审核实践时，审核效应依然存在。

Conclusion: 本文推进了社交媒体广告情境效应理论，首次提出避免"负面邻近"的针对性预防性干预。对管理者而言，AI辅助评论审核能影响真实广告表现，但可能取决于平台层面的透明度设计。

Abstract: Social media advertising exposes brands not only to potential customers but also to unfiltered consumer discourse in the form of user comments. While comments can enhance authenticity and engagement, they also introduce reputational risks through spam, hate speech, and negative user-generated content. Despite the increasing prevalence of AI-powered comment moderation solutions, little causal evidence exists on whether moderation (i.e., hiding harmful comments) improves ad effectiveness. Across six empirical studies-including two large-scale field experiments and four online studies-we demonstrate that automated moderation of harmful comments causally improves ad performance, including conversion rates, return on ad spend, and purchase intentions. We also identify two important platform-governance boundary conditions: the gains from moderation depend on whether the platform is transparent about the brand's moderation behavior, and what types of comments are moderated. At the same time, the moderation effect persists when the brand is transparent about its own moderation practices. We advance theory on context effects in social media advertising, by uncovering the first targeted, preventative intervention for avoiding negative adjacencies. For managers, the results show that AI-assisted comment moderation impacts real ad performance but may be contingent upon platform-level transparency design.

</details>


### [176] [Social Behavior Among Autonomous AI: How Large Language Models Interact in Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16013)
*Narges Fardnia,Fatemeh Seyedin,Matthias Becker,Mahmoudreza Babaei,Adrian Weller*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究考察了基于LLM的AI（Mistral、Llama3、Gemma3、Phi3）在动态网络结构公共物品博弈中的合作行为，发现模型架构、网络拓扑和提示设计显著影响合作水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作是人类社会的基石，而随着AI系统自主行动，理解人-AI合作及AI-AI在自适应网络中的互动变得至关重要。

Method: 在动态网络结构（WS、BA、ER三种网络）中开展公共物品博弈，采用单模型与混合模型条件，使用四种LLM（Mistral、Llama3、Gemma3、Phi3），分析模型架构、网络拓扑与提示设计对合作行为的影响。

Result: Mistral和Llama3合作率高，Phi3表现出背叛倾向；ER网络的随机结构显著提升合作；提示设计也起关键作用，强调社会利益的提示能带来更高合作水平。

Conclusion: 研究为基于LLM的自适应社会网络模拟提供了一个初步框架。

Abstract: Cooperation is a cornerstone of human societies, enabling collective progress in dynamic and uncertain environments. With the advent of AI systems acting autonomously, it becomes crucial to understand not only human-AI cooperation but also AI-AI interactions in adaptive networks. In this work, we examine the interactions of AI using Large Language Models -- Mistral, Llama3, Gemma3, and Phi3 -- in a public goods game within dynamic network structures. Our experiments were conducted under single-model and mixed-model conditions across Watts-Strogatz (WS), Barabasi-Albert (BA), and Erdos-Renyi (ER) networks. We analyzed the impact of model architecture, network topology, and prompt design on cooperative behavior. Results show that Mistral and Llama3 offer high cooperation rates, while Phi3 shows defective tendencies. Additionally, the random structure of Erdos-Renyi networks dramatically improves cooperation. Prompt design also plays a key role; a society-benefits prompt leads to a higher cooperation level. These findings offer a preliminary framework for LLM-based simulations in adaptive social networks.

</details>


### [177] [Digital Persuasion: Understanding the Impact of Online Influencers on Public Opinion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16062)
*Omran Berjawi,Rida Khatoun,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Friedkin-Johnsen (FJ)模型的意见动力学框架，用于识别社交网络中的影响力用户并研究其对社区意见的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究意见动力学及其在社交网络中的传播，对应对政治极化、公共卫生和营销策略等挑战至关重要。

Method: 基于FJ模型（假设个体有初始意见和表达意见两种），通过一系列初始意见操纵实验，比较影响力用户与随机用户对整体社区意见的影响；并用代表美国总统大选的推文数据集进行验证。

Result: 影响力得分最高的影响者可显著改变整体社区意见；其影响不仅限于直接邻居，还会扩展到邻居的邻居。

Conclusion: 该研究表明社交媒体上的数字影响者能够就某一议题或事业塑造公众意见。

Abstract: The studying of opinion dynamics and its propagation within social networks is crucial for addressing a wide range of challenges, including political polarization, public health, and marketing strategies. In this work, we study the problem of opinion dynamics by proposing a framework based on Friedkin-Johnsen (FJ) to identifies influential users and study their impact on dynamics opinions of community. The FJ model assume each individual have two opinions: initial and expressed. Through a series of initial opinion manipulation experiments, the proposed framework assesses the impact of influential versus random users on the overall community opinion. The proposed framework is validated using a tweet dataset representing the U.S. presidential election. The results shows that influencers with highest influencing score, significantly shift the overall community opinion. Moreover, the results shows that the impact of influencers not limited to direct neighbors , but beyond it, to their neighbors of neighbors . This study demonstrates how digital influencers on social media can shape public opinion regarding a subject or cause.

</details>


### [178] [Predicting Social Media Engagement using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16082)
*Ritwik Singh,Mayukh Majumdar,Subodha Kumar*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究从Facebook家具企业图像帖子中提取视觉、文本和时间特征，用多种机器学习模型评估社交媒体参与度的驱动因素与预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体是重要信息传播和广告渠道，企业通过优质帖子提升互动指标和粉丝数。已有研究多聚焦社交媒体互动，但较少联合考察图像帖子的视觉、文本和时间特征，而这些特征共同决定内容表现。

Method: 收集Facebook上家具企业的图像帖子，使用文本和图像分析方法提取视觉、时间、文本特征；评估Random Forest、LightGBM、XGBoost等机器学习模型，分析参与度驱动因素及预测能力。

Result: 量化了这些特征与用户互动之间的关联程度，并展示了所评估机器学习模型对社交媒体参与度的预测能力。

Conclusion: 视觉、文本和时间特征共同影响社交媒体参与度，研究为企业优化社交媒体内容提供了可参考的建议。

Abstract: Social media platforms are popular channels for disseminating information, owing to their large user bases and ease of access. Companies also use social media as an important aspect of the advertising process. By creating high-quality posts, companies can strengthen their engagement metrics and increase their follower count. While a growing body of research has examined social media engagement, fewer studies have jointly examined the visual, textual, and temporal features of image posts, even though these features collectively determine the performance of content on social media. To understand the important drivers of social media engagement, we collect image posts of furniture firms on Facebook and extract visual, temporal, and textual features from them using text and image analytics methods. We evaluate several machine learning models - including Random Forest, Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) - to assess the drivers and the prediction power of social media engagement using the features from our data. Our research quantifies the extent to which these features are associated with interactions and provides recommendations that organizations may consider.

</details>


### [179] [netseg: a Python Package for Measuring Structural Polarization and Segregation in Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16088)
*Onur Tuncay Bal,Michał Bojanowski*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 netseg，一个文档完善、经过测试的 Python 包，实现社会网络结构极化与隔离的经典网络指标，多数指标推广至多群体及有向图，性能较现有开源实现快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构极化与隔离研究依赖一组广泛引用的网络指标，但其实现代码极少公开、几乎从未测试，且缺乏统一、高效的软件工具。

Method: netseg 移植同名 R 包并新增随机游走争议度、边界连通性、偶极矩、Moran's I 等指标；基于 igraph 对象，通过其 Python 接口以编译代码执行邻域查询与随机游走模拟；支持任意零模型图集合，并区分已含基线的指标以避免重复扣减。

Result: 多个指标运行时间比现有开源实现低数个数量级（文档报告基准）；对生成式观点模型做参数扫描给出各指标行为，并应用于十九世纪运营商记录结合全量普查数据构建的县级铁路网络。

Conclusion: netseg 为结构极化与隔离指标提供可靠、高效、文档齐全且可测试的实现，支持自定义零模型并适用于真实网络数据分析。

Abstract: The study of structural polarization and segregation in social networks is an established line of research, and the quantification of both phenomena proceeds through a set of widely cited network indices. The code implementing those indices, however, is seldom released and almost never tested. We present netseg, a comprehensively documented Python package implementing these indices, most of them generalized to more than two groups and to directed as well as undirected input. It ports the R package of the same name and adds measures the R version lacks, among them Random Walk Controversy, Boundary Connectivity, Dipole Moment, and Moran's I. The package operates on igraph objects and performs the underlying graph operations (e.g., neighborhood queries and random-walk simulation) through igraph's Python interface, so that they execute in compiled code rather than in interpreted Python. For several of the indices this yields runtimes orders of magnitude below those of the available open-source implementations, which the documentation reports in benchmarks. Most of these indices are defined as a divergence from a null model, and published implementations fix that null model to a uniform random graph of matching density. netseg accepts an ensemble of graphs as a sample from an arbitrary null model, and distinguishes indices that already incorporate a baseline from those that do not, adjusting the comparison accordingly to avoid double subtraction. The documentation provides, for each index, a worked empirical example, its behaviour at the degenerate cases where it is undefined, a benchmark, and the procedure for substituting a custom null model. We report the behaviour of every index over a parameter sweep of a generative opinion model, and apply them to a county-level railroad network built from nineteenth-century operator records joined to full-count census data.

</details>


### [180] [On the Expressive Power of Implicit Line-Graph Higher-Order Weisfeiler--Leman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16412)
*Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式线图WL (ILG-k-WL)，在根图边上直接执行线图k-WL而不显式构造L(G)，研究线图与根图上WL表达力的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whitney定理将连通简单图（除K3和K1,3外）的同构检测转化为线图区分，但线图与根图上固定维WL表达力的关系尚不明确。

Method: 定义ILG-k-WL：在G的边上执行k-WL，线图关系由端点关联导出，不显式构造L(G)。在Whitney-general类上分析根域与线图WL的关系，并证明k=3时的反向包含，扩展到无孤立点非连通图。

Result: k=1,2时ILG-k-WL无额外区分力；k=3时证明L(G)≡3-WL L(H) ⇒ G≡3-WL H。ILG-3-WL严格强于3-WL（强正则见证对如Shrikhande/rook）。分离全部3个子结构计数对、SR25全部105对、BREC 400对中的359对；未训练稠密ILG-3-GNN给出相同判定。

Conclusion: ILG-3-WL在Whitney-general类上比根域3-WL更强且反向包含成立，为线图与根图WL表达力关系提供了新理解，并具GNN实现潜力。

Abstract: Whitney's theorem allows isomorphism testing for connected simple graphs, apart from $K_3$ and $K_{1,3}$, to be formulated as distinguishing their line graphs. However, the relation between fixed-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman (WL) expressivity on line graphs and on their roots remains unresolved. We study this relation through Implicit Line-Graph WL (ILG-$k$-WL), which is exactly $k$-WL on $L(G)$, executed over the edges of $G$ with line-graph relations derived from endpoint incidence and without explicitly constructing $L(G)$. On the Whitney-general class, the relation between root-domain and line-graph WL depends on $k$. For $k=1,2$, ILG-$k$-WL adds no distinguishing power beyond root-domain $1$-WL and misses some pairs that $1$-WL separates. For $k=3$, we prove the backward containment $L(G)\equiv_{3\text{-WL}}L(H)\Rightarrow G\equiv_{3\text{-WL}}H$. Strongly regular witness pairs, including the Shrikhande/rook pair, show that ILG-$3$-WL is strictly more expressive than $3$-WL. The backward containment also extends to disconnected graphs with no isolated vertices when every connected component is Whitney-general. Deterministic ILG-$3$-WL separates all three substructure-counting witness pairs, all $105$ pairs in SR25, and $359$ of $400$ BREC pairs. An untrained dense ILG-$3$-GNN gives the same pairwise verdicts on these evaluations.

</details>


### [181] [Inferring Temporal Dependencies from Social Time Series with the Cross-Correlogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16633)
*Bridget Smart,Renaud Lambiotte,Takaaki Aoki,Ryota Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于平滑强度函数校正的互相关图方法，用于在周期性、爆发性社会系统中稳健估计时间依赖剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会行为具有爆发性和非平稳性，违反许多时间依赖度量方法的平稳性假设。现有方法（如Granger因果、共现）或需强假设，或仅给出单一汇总统计，难以刻画完整的滞后依赖结构。

Method: 将行为功能模型与数据驱动的时间响应剖面结合，改进传统互相关图：分析周期结构对互相关图的偏差，提出基于平滑强度函数的校正（可由已知函数形式指定或经验估计）。

Result: 理论上和仿真中，该方法在周期性情境下能恢复时间依赖，优于间隔抖动法和Granger因果法。应用于2019–2020年310万条X（推特）事件时间，揭示了共现度量遗漏的延迟时间关系；对电视相关话题标签，恢复的延迟与已知播出时间表吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了稳健、可解释的时间依赖剖面估计器，在集体节律与目标交互时间尺度重叠时仍有效，能捕捉真实的集体在线行为时间结构而非共享注意力周期的伪影。

Abstract: Characterizing temporal interactions in social systems is challenging because social behavior can be bursty and non-stationary, violating the stationarity assumptions of many methods used to measure temporal dependence. The cross-correlogram, an existing technique used to profile neural excitations and inhibitions, offers an interpretable alternative to methods such as Granger causality or co-occurrence, as it produces a full profile of lagged dependence directly from event times rather than a single summary statistic. We adapt the cross-correlogram by integrating functional models of behavior with data-driven temporal response profiling. By characterizing how periodic structure biases traditional cross-correlograms, we propose a correction based on smooth intensity functions, specified from a known functional form or estimated empirically. This approach provides a robust, interpretable estimator of temporal dependency profiles even when collective rhythms operate on timescales that overlap those of the interactions of interest. We demonstrate theoretically and through simulation that the proposed method recovers temporal dependencies in periodic regimes, outperforming interval-jitter and Granger causality methods. Finally, we apply the method to 3.1 million event times from X (formerly Twitter) collected between 2019 and 2020, demonstrating how cross-correlograms reveal delayed temporal relationships in collective online behavior that are missed by co-occurrence measures. For a subset of television-related hashtags, recovered delays align with known broadcast schedules, providing evidence that the proposed method captures genuine temporal structure rather than artifacts of shared attention cycles.

</details>


### [182] [The Local-to-Global AD-k Conjecture is Resolved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16663)
*Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文证明了Chen等人提出的猜想：在一般阈值模型中，若每个局部影响函数是AD-k的，则全局影响扩散函数也是AD-k的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen等人猜想一般阈值模型中局部影响函数的AD-k性质可传递到全局影响扩散函数，但此前缺乏完整证明，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 利用Möbius反演和决策树划分方法，并将节点触发集的概率分布扩展为允许触发集具有负权重的广义代数结构。

Result: 完整证明了该猜想对任意（可能含环）有向图和任意k均成立。

Conclusion: 该猜想成立，且负权重触发集的推广结构可能具有独立研究价值。

Abstract: AD-k stands for Alternating Differences through order k, and it is a property of set functions denoting that the first order difference of the set function is nonnegative (a.k.a. monotnocity), the second order difference is nonpositive (submodularity), and so on with signs alternating through order k. Chen et al. [1] conjectured that in an influence diffusion model called the general threshold model originally defined by Kempe et al. [2], if every local influence function is AD-k, then the global influence spread function is also AD-k, for any (possibly cyclic) directed graph and any k. This paper provides a complete proof showing that the conjecture is true. The proof utilizes Mobius inversion and decision tree partition method and extends the probability distribution of node triggering sets into a generalized algebraic structure allowing negative weights for triggering sets. The extension to negative-weighted triggering sets may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [183] [WCCS: Efficient Wedge Conductance Community Search over Large Temporal Bipartite Graphs (Full Paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16882)
*Longlong Lin,Wei Chen,Pingpeng Yuan,Ruikun Luo,Qiangqiang Dai,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出"楔形电导社区搜索(WCCS)"问题，通过高阶(α,β,τ)-楔核与时间楔形电导度量，在非连续时间戳上寻找查询相关社区，并提供在线过滤-扩展框架与离线压缩索引两种高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分图社区搜索已有大量研究，但多数工作局限于静态场景，忽视时间动态；且现有方法多采用边中心度量与严格连续性约束，无法捕捉高阶交互与非连续但频繁的活动；更关键的是忽略了社区质量需同时满足内部紧密性与外部稀疏性，导致关键节点遗漏或纳入大量无关节点。

Method: 将经典(α,β)-核推广为高阶(α,β,τ)-楔核，并提出时间楔形电导度量以平衡内部密度与外部稀疏性，形式化WCCS问题；求解上先提出在线优先级驱动的过滤-扩展框架，结合有效剪枝与几何斜率优化快速计算时间楔形电导；进一步提出离线压缩索引以提升可扩展性。

Result: 在七个真实数据集上的综合实验表明，所提方案在有效性、效率与可扩展性上均优于八个对比方法。

Conclusion: WCCS为时间二分图中的社区搜索提供了兼顾结构与时序、内部紧密与外部稀疏的新范式，在线与离线两种算法验证了其可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Bipartite graphs are ubiquitous for modeling complex interactions between two distinct entity types across numerous practical applications such as e-commerce, academic networks, and social systems. Despite significant progress in community search over bipartite graphs, most prior work is limited to static settings and ignores the rich temporal dynamics present in real-world networks. Moreover, existing methods typically adopt edge-centric measures and strict consecutivity constraints, failing to capture higher-order interactions and frequent yet non-consecutive activities. More importantly, they often neglect the crucial community-quality requirements of both internal cohesiveness and external sparsity, failing to identify critical nodes or including many irrelevant nodes. To address these dilemmas, we propose the novel problem of \emph{Wedge Conductance Community Search (WCCS)}, which aims to identify a query-dependent community that is not only structurally and temporally cohesive but also well-separated from the rest of the network over non-consecutive timestamps. We formalize WCCS by generalizing the classical $(α,β)$-core to a higher-order $(α,β,τ)$-wedge core, and by proposing a novel temporal wedge conductance metric that explicitly balances internal density and external sparsity.
  To solve WCCS efficiently, we first develop an online priority-driven filter-and-expand framework with several effective pruning techniques and a powerful geometric slope optimization for rapid temporal wedge conductance calculation. Subsequently, to further improve scalability, we propose an offline compressed index to accelerate search. Finally, comprehensive experiments on seven real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability of our solutions compared to eight competitors.

</details>


### [184] [Graphlets as structural fingerprints of complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17445)
*Anna Pidnebesna,David Hartman,Aneta Pokorna,Daniel Trlifaj,Jaroslav Hlinka*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于graphlet（小根子图分布）的结构指纹框架，用于系统刻画复杂网络的局部到介观拓扑，并评估其相较经典图论度量的优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对比复杂网络多用选定的单一图论度量（如度、聚类系数、介数中心性），只能捕捉部分局部到全局属性，缺乏系统性描述，因此需要更全面的结构指纹方法。

Method: 构建基于graphlet分布的指纹框架；在多种随机图模型生成的合成网络上检验其捕捉参数依赖结构差异的能力；与标准图论度量对比并识别驱动区分的关键局部模式；再应用于静息态功能连接组，包括受控拓扑扰动及精神分裂症-对照分类。

Result: 在合成网络上，graphlet指纹能捕捉参数依赖的结构差异，优于标准图论度量，并能识别驱动区分的细微局部模式；在功能连接组中，对受控拓扑扰动更敏感，但在精神分裂症-对照分类上仅与经典图论特征相当。

Conclusion: graphlet可作为灵活的结构指纹，但需谨慎看待其强弱：其优势在局部/介观结构刻画与扰动敏感性，而真实疾病分类中未必优于经典特征，这与精神分裂症改变以空间局部连接变化为主、而非整体拓扑重组的观点一致。

Abstract: Complex networks are often compared using selected graph-theoretical measures that capture a selected set of properties with effects ranging from local to global, such as degree, clustering or betweenness centrality. Here we introduce a structural fingerprinting framework based on graphlets: small rooted subgraphs whose distributions provide a systematic description of local-to-mesoscale topology. Across synthetic networks generated from several random graph models, graphlet fingerprints capture parameter-dependent structural differences, outperform standard graph-theoretical measures, and identify even subtle local patterns driving discrimination. We then apply the framework to empirical resting-state functional connectomes, documenting that while graphlets show superior sensitivity also to controlled topological perturbations of brain connectivity, specifically in schizophrenia-control classification they perform only comparably to classical graph-theoretical features. This is in line with the notion that schizophrenia-related alterations are dominated by spatially localized connectivity changes rather than general topological reorganization. Altogether, the generative modeling, targeted perturbations and real-world neuroimaging classification challenge position graphlets as flexible structural fingerprints of complex networks, while carefully outlining their strength and weaknesses compared to more classical graph theoretical features.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Optimal Pruning for Neural Architectures using Fisher Information Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16129)
*David S. Berman,Yen-Yu Fu,Edward Hirst,Thelma Chiwete Obirai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于模型空间中微分几何距离（Fisher信息度量下的测地距离）的参数剪枝新方法，构建了从幅度剪枝到更优剪枝方案的层级体系，并在多种架构和数据集上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统剪枝方法（如幅度剪枝）缺乏数学理论依据，无法准确衡量剪枝对模型真实性能的影响。作者希望从微分几何角度给出剪枝的理论基础，定义参数置零所对应的模型位移，并用测地距离刻画剪枝引起的真实模型变化。

Method: 将剪枝视为模型移动到参数为零的超曲面，用Fisher信息度量下的测地距离计算从原模型到该超曲面的最短距离作为剪枝影响度量。通过逐步逼近该测地距离，构造出一系列最优性层级：从传统幅度剪枝到更复杂有效的新剪枝方案，并分析不同近似层次的效率与性能权衡。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，针对全连接网络和视觉Transformer，在0-100%完整剪枝范围及5个随机种子上测试。新方法在准确率和Matthews相关系数上均优于幅度剪枝和局部Fisher信息剪枝。同时，中间层次的测地近似方案计算高效且保持近最优性能。

Conclusion: 该几何框架不仅提供了最先进的AI模型剪枝方法，还为剪枝方案提供了经过验证且具数学动机的理论依据，统一并推广了现有剪枝方法。

Abstract: A new scheme for parameter pruning is introduced, derived from the differential-geometric distance in model space. Pruning a parameter sets its value to zero, representing a displacement of the model to the hypersurface on which that parameter vanishes. The minimal distance from the unpruned model to this hypersurface is naturally computed via the geodesic distance in the model space as determined by the Fisher information metric. This distance determines the true change in the model, and its performance, under pruning. By analysing progressively more faithful approximations of this geodesic distance a natural hierarchy of optimality for pruning methods is determined. This starts with the traditional magnitude pruning, then develops into new more sophisticated and effective pruning schemes. The method is demonstrated for both fully-connected networks and vision transformers, on MNIST and CIFAR-10, over the complete $0$-$100\%$ pruning range and across five random seeds. It outperforms pruning by parameter magnitude and by the local Fisher information alone in every architecture and dataset combination considered, on both accuracy and the Matthews correlation coefficient. Additionally, analysis of different levels of geodesic approximation produces intermediate pruning schemes that are computationally efficient and maintain near-optimal performance. This geometric picture supplies not only a state-of-the-art pruning methodology for AI models, but also a verified and mathematically-motivated justification for pruning schemes.

</details>


### [186] [Safe Error Correction for Language Models: Frozen-Base Adjustment with Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16145)
*Gautam Kishore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出轻量logit级修正模块CRN v2，仅0.73%可训练参数、冻结基座模型，在CEHRI考试中修正53.3%的基座错误且不损失基座能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究小修正模块能否在不损害冻结语言模型基座能力的前提下修正其输出错误，并揭示"修正-能力"权衡这一核心设计问题。

Method: 在完全冻结的Gemma 4 E2B上叠加~34M可训练参数的logit级修正模块，仅训练修正模块；采用SFT+无参考DPO（83,400错误修正对）；加入KL保持项(λ=0.1)锚定基座；并做LoRA、隐状态注入、多深度、更长训练等多组消融对比。

Result: CRN v2修正53.3%基座错误（改写变体43.3%），在MMLU/BoolQ(N=200)与car-wash(N=8)上无退化；匹配预算LoRA(6.6M,rank19)修正83.3%但能力损失30-75%；KL项降至0.01修正降至35.0%；早期层隐状态注入50.0%/55.8%、层4降至30.0%/28.3%、多深度~40%、延长训练仍53.3%，均未超过rank-128 logit结果。

Conclusion: 该工作验证"冻结基座+logit修正+KL锚定"的设计原则（非架构创新）；~53%为迄今最佳结果而非下限；代码、主结果权重与评测脚本已开源。

Abstract: We study a practical question: can a small correction module fix errors in a frozen language model's outputs without degrading its base capabilities? We propose CRN v2, a lightweight logit-level correction module (~34M trainable parameters, 0.73% of the 4.65B text module) that sits atop a fully frozen Gemma 4 E2B model. The base model is never updated; only the correction module learns, via supervised fine-tuning followed by reference-free DPO on 83,400 error-correction pairs. On a 60-question domain exam (CEHRI: Certified Human-Robot Intelligence, covering facts, arithmetic, and implicit-goal reasoning), CRN v2 corrects 53.3% of base-model errors (reworded variant: 43.3%) while showing no degradation on tested capability benchmarks (MMLU/BoolQ N=200; car-wash N=8). A LoRA baseline at the matched CRN v1 budget (6.6M params, rank 19) achieves 83.3% correction but suffers 30-75% capability loss on the same benchmarks -- the correction-capability tradeoff. An ablation shows that the KL preservation term (lambda=0.1) is critical: lowering it to 0.01 degrades correction to 35.0%. A hidden-state injection variant at earlier layers (1.6M params, SFT-only) reaches 50.0%/55.8% but does not exceed logit correction; shallower injection (layer 4) drops to 30.0%/28.3%; multi-depth logit correction (~35M) reaches only 40%; and longer training (5,000 SFT + 2,000 DPO) stays at 53.3% -- none of the alternative configurations we tested exceeded the rank-128 logit result, consistent with a best-achieved result of ~53% rather than a floor. This is a study of a design principle (frozen base + logit correction + KL anchoring), not a claim of architectural novelty. All code, main-result weights, and evaluation scripts are released (deep variant as code only -- no trained deep checkpoints).

</details>


### [187] [GPEvac: GNN-Based PPO for Adaptive Evacuation Routing During Shooting Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16163)
*Daniel Perkins,Subhadeep Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPEvac：基于GNN+PPO的自适应疏散路径规划框架，可在14.73ms内计算全局疏散路线，跨不同建筑布局显著降低威胁暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模枪击事件激增，亟需实时引导受害者避险的系统。有效疏散需兼顾威胁暴露最小化、对抗性不确定性与拥挤动态。现有方法局限于特定布局策略且大规模下计算不可行，实用指南仅建议"跑、躲、打"。

Method: 提出GPEvac，一种基于GNN的PPO框架。采用边优先的顺序消息传递机制并引入可学习的虚拟全局节点，以捕获局部与长距离依赖；将图嵌入整合进置换不变的评分机制，使单一策略可跨不同拓扑与规模的建筑布局运行。

Result: 大量仿真表明，GPEvac在多种建筑布局上优于智能基线，显著降低总威胁暴露；在本地CPU上仅用14.73ms即可计算全局疏散路线，可与实时监控系统无缝集成。

Conclusion: GPEvac可提升枪击事件中的生存率，其方法亦可迁移至关键基础设施、智能交通系统与自适应传感器网络等图结构决策领域。

Abstract: The sharp increase in mass shootings underscores an urgent need for systems that guide victims to safety in real time. An effective evacuation system must minimize threat exposure while also accounting for adversarial uncertainty and crowding dynamics. Current methods in the literature are rigidly constrained to layout-specific policies and computationally intractable in large-scale layouts, while practical guidelines simply advise victims to "run", "hide", or "fight". We propose GPEvac: a GNN-based PPO framework that computes adaptive evacuation routes during shooting events. To capture both local and long-distance dependencies, we introduce an edge-first sequential message-passing scheme with a learnable virtual global node. The resulting graph embeddings are integrated into a permutation-invariant scoring mechanism that allows a single learned policy to operate across building layouts of diverse topologies and sizes. Through extensive simulation, we show that GPEvac outperforms intelligent baselines across distinct architectural layouts, significantly reducing total threat exposure. Crucially, the system computes global evacuation routes in just 14.73 ms on local CPU hardware, enabling seamless integration with live surveillance systems.
