The comparison therefore treats efficiency and isolation as a design trade-off rather than declaring one technology universally superior. A hybrid architecture, in which containers run inside hardened VMs, often provides a practical balance for cloud and multi-tenant systems.

</details>


### [259] [Models as Governed Interfaces for AI-Native MBSE: Read-Side Adequacy and Write-Side Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16252)
*Jason Gower,Michael J. de C. Henshaw,Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 机器可读的SysML v2模型虽可编程访问，但AI参与系统工程的障碍在于数据架构而非建模语言。作者以Apollo 11模型为例，提出"认知充分性"（epistemic adequacy）属性，分读写两侧共五条准则，并提出治理查询架构框架来规范AI参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器可读模型（如SysML v2）的可编程访问被视为AI参与系统工程的前提，但仅有访问不够。AI查询结构完整的模型时仍会遇到推导链缺失、认知状态未标注、溯源缺失和无法解析的证据等缺口；此时AI不会弃答，而是用既不可验证也不受治理的训练数据填补缺口。因此，问题不在建模语言，而在其周围的数据架构。

Method: 以一个按当前实践堪称典范（而非有缺陷）的模型为案例，探查公开的Apollo 11 SysML v2重建。提出"认知充分性"作为候选数据架构模式，分两半：读侧充分性（让推导、状态和溯源能回答查询而非诱导猜测）和写侧可准入性（在AI贡献进入记录前进行把关）。该属性分解为五条准则：四条在读侧，由案例与趋同文献佐证；第五条在参与侧，作为本文尚未检验的假设提出。架构空间从内联元数据扩展至原生多模型存储，并据此提出"治理查询架构框架"，通过工程师已使用的视点约定来治理智能体参与。

Result: 文章将重构置于可证伪框架下：若认知层无法在同一模型上击败检索增强基线，则该层即被证伪。检验先在Apollo推导链上进行，随后在工业试点中开展。

Conclusion: AI参与系统工程的真正瓶颈在于数据架构而非建模语言。作者提出认知充分性属性和治理查询架构框架，为规范AI参与提供方向，并将该重构作为可证伪主张加以检验。

Abstract: Machine-readable models such as SysML v2 are now programmatically accessible, and a growing body of work treats that access as the enabling condition for AI participation in systems engineering. Access is necessary, but not sufficient. The remaining work lies not in the modelling language but in the data architecture around it. An AI reader that queries a structurally complete model for a derivation still runs into absent derivation chains, untagged epistemic status, missing provenance, and evidence that the model cannot resolve. Faced with these gaps, it does not abstain; it fills them from training data, a source that is neither verifiable nor governed. To make the case on a model that is exemplary by current practice rather than deficient, we probe the public Apollo 11 SysML v2 reconstruction. We name the missing property epistemic adequacy and offer it as a candidate data-architecture pattern in two halves. Read-side adequacy lets derivation, status, and provenance answer a query rather than invite a guess; write-side admissibility gates an AI contribution before it enters the record. The property is broken down into five criteria. Four sit on the read side, evidenced by the case and convergent literature; the fifth sits on the participation side, advanced as a hypothesis this paper does not yet test. The architecture space runs from an inline metadata extension up to a substrate-native multi-model store, and over it, we propose the Governed-Query Architecture Framework, which governs agent participation through the viewpoint conventions that engineers already use. We commit the reframing to falsification: the epistemic layer counts as refuted if it cannot beat a retrieval-augmented baseline on the same model, tested first on the Apollo chain and then in an industrial pilot.

</details>


### [260] [AgentGuard: Learning Execution Guardrails from Anomalous Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16287)
*Wuyang Dai,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentGuard：从历史失败轨迹中自动学习指令级行为约束，动态激活相关规则来提升编码智能体执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体依赖执行框架与仓库、外部工具交互，但任务成功不代表执行可靠，仍会出现修改无关文件、重写测试、危险命令、忽略校验失败等问题，因此需要行为护栏。

Method: 提出指令级护栏框架AgentGuard，不依赖手工规则，而是自动从异常轨迹中提取反复出现的执行失败模式，泛化为指令级行为约束，并组织为轻量级护栏技能，仅动态激活与当前指令相关的规则。

Result: 基于382个仓库任务收集642条失败轨迹，用461条（282任务）学习、100个不相交任务评估；Claude Code + Claude Haiku 4.5下，异常执行率从69.0%降至26.7%，任务成功率从21.7%提升至35.0%。

Conclusion: 从历史失败中学习的执行护栏能显著提升AI编码智能体的可靠性，但仍需解决安全性与任务完成之间的平衡问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on execution harnesses to interact with repositories and external tools. However, task success does not guarantee reliable execution. Agents may still modify unrelated files, rewrite tests, issue unsafe commands, or ignore failed validations, motivating behavioral guardrails for reliable execution. We present AgentGuard, an instruction-level guardrail framework that learns conditional execution constraints from anomalous trajectories of coding agents. Rather than relying on manually specified safety rules, AgentGuard automatically extracts recurring execution failure patterns, generalizes them into instruction-level behavioral constraints, and organizes them as a lightweight guardrail skill that dynamically activates only the rules relevant to the current instruction.
  This design enables behavioral guidance while minimizing unnecessary restrictions on normal execution. We evaluate AgentGuard using 642 documented failure traces collected from real coding-agent executions across 382 repository tasks. Guardrails are learned from 461 traces covering 282 tasks and evaluated on a disjoint set of 100 tasks. Using Claude Code with Claude Haiku 4.5 as the underlying coding agent, we compare the baseline agent with the same agent augmented by AgentGuard. Experimental results show that AgentGuard reduces the Abnormal Execution Rate from 69.0% to 26.7% and increases the Successful Task Completion Rate from 21.7% to 35.0%. These results demonstrate that execution guardrails learned from historical failures can substantially improve the reliability of AI coding agents while highlighting the remaining challenge of balancing safety and task completion.

</details>


### [261] [Assurance Envelopes for Autonomous Coding Agents: Minimum-Cost Evidence for Software Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16302)
*Anjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将"变更需恢复哪些已有证据"建模为带类型推理图上的最小成本子集选择问题（任务条件保证包络），用前向闭包验证、CP-SAT求解，并验证其在不同任务下的依赖性与结构驱动难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理回到既有软件时继承了大量早前证据（测试、类型检查、证明、静态分析、trace）。全部重载浪费，但丢弃变更所依赖的某块证据又可能使必需属性失去支撑。因此需要一个按"变更必须保持的属性"（义务）来选取最低成本证据子集的方法。

Method: 把证据与组合规则建成带类型的推理图；义务被满足定义为从所选证据前向链式推理可达该义务。用该闭包（而非直接信任优化器）验证每次选择。数据为先前AI编码代理运行留下的冻结产物，问题即"后续任务应恢复哪些累积证据"，用CP-SAT精确求解。

Result: Rust、IronBlocks、Pong 小图显示：最小包络随任务变化；当前证据无法重建必需属性时可能无解；某些属性需多块证据联合支撑；需求扩大只会增加而非替换证据。249 个预设合成基准刻画计算性：忽略"多证据联合"结构的基线必然无法重导这些属性；所有完成的精确交叉检验与 CP-SAT 一致；500 证据图的中位求解时间低于 20ms，但每个目标有大量替代推导的图在更小规模即超时——难度由结构而非原始规模决定。

Conclusion: 贡献在于把已有优化方法有界地应用于为软件变更选取保证上下文；如何发现义务及对下游代理的实际收益仍是未解问题。

Abstract: When a coding agent returns to existing software, it inherits evidence from earlier engineering work: tests, type checks, proofs, static analyses, and traces. Reloading all of it is wasteful, but dropping a piece the change depends on can leave a required property unsupported. Given the properties a change must preserve, its obligations, we ask which least-cost subset of the available evidence re-establishes them, and we call such a subset a task-conditioned assurance envelope. Evidence and the rules that combine it form a typed inference graph; an obligation is met when forward chaining from the selected evidence reaches it, and we validate every selection by that closure rather than by trusting the optimizer. The software-derived graphs in our evaluation come from preserved outcomes of prior AI coding-agent runs; we freeze those artifacts and ask which accumulated evidence should be restored for a later task. Small graphs from Rust, IronBlocks, and Pong outcomes show that the minimum envelope depends on the task, that none may exist when current evidence cannot re-establish a required property, that some properties need several pieces of evidence together, and that expanding the requirements adds evidence rather than replacing it. A prespecified synthetic benchmark of 249 instances characterizes computation: a baseline that discards the 'several pieces together' structure necessarily fails to re-derive them; every completed exact cross-check agreed with the CP-SAT optimizer; and median solve time stayed below 20 ms at 500-evidence graphs, except that graphs with many alternative derivations per target timed out at far smaller sizes, so structure, not raw size, drives difficulty. The contribution is a bounded application of established optimization to selecting assurance context for a software change; discovering the obligations and downstream agent benefit remain open.

</details>


### [262] [FairLint-DL: An IDE-Native Tool for Fairness Debugging of Deep Learning Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16321)
*Archit Rathod,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FairLint-DL 是一个 VS Code 扩展，将公平性测试左移，在预训练阶段直接在表格数据集上检测偏差，通过代理模型与 QID 度量实现快速（<12秒）的公平性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性分析工具多为训练后评估框架，需完成整个模型开发流程才能评估偏差，无法在开发早期介入。

Method: 训练可配置深度神经网络作为代理模型；基于 Shannon 与 min-entropy 的信息论 QID 指标；两阶段梯度引导搜索算法发现歧视实例；通过敏感性分析定位偏差到具体网络层和神经元；SHAP 与 LIME 双解释引擎做特征归因。

Result: 在 Adult、German Credit、Bank Marketing 三个表格基准上评估。Adult 数据集上 96.0% 实例 QID 超 0.1-bit 阈值，平均 QID 0.619 bits，差异化影响比 0.581，违反四分之五法则；缓存模型上 12 秒内完成分析。

Conclusion: FairLint-DL 证明可将公平性分析集成到开发者工作流中且开销可控，实现 IDE 原生的左移公平性测试。

Abstract: Existing fairness analysis tools predominantly operate as post-training evaluation frameworks, requiring practitioners to complete the full model development lifecycle before assessing bias. We present FairLint-DL, a Visual Studio Code extension that implements a shift-left approach to fairness testing by enabling pre-training, IDE-native bias detection directly on tabular datasets. FairLint-DL trains a configurable deep neural network as a proxy model and applies information-theoretic Quantitative Individual Discrimination (QID) metrics. Grounded in Shannon and min-entropy, QID quantifies the causal influence of protected attributes on predictions. The system implements a two-phase gradient-guided search algorithm for discovering discriminatory instances, a causal debugging pipeline that localizes bias to specific network layers and neurons via sensitivity analysis, and dual explainability engines using SHAP and LIME for feature-level attribution. Evaluation on three tabular benchmarks (Adult Census Income, German Credit, and Bank Marketing) reveals fairness concerns that vary widely across datasets: on Adult, 96.0% of analyzed instances exhibit QID above the 0.1-bit significance threshold, with a mean QID of 0.619 bits and a disparate impact ratio of 0.581, violating the four-fifths legal rule. FairLint-DL produces these results within 12 seconds on cached models, demonstrating the feasibility of integrating fairness analysis into the developer workflow without significant overhead.

</details>


### [263] [Protocol-Preserving Context Trimming for Agentic Workflows: Benefits, Failure Regimes, and Budget Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16461)
*Harish Gaggar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估"协议保持型上下文裁剪"作为多步智能体工作流中受可靠性约束的方法。在五种裁剪策略中，自适应预算护栏表现最佳：96.0%任务成功率、96.3%协议遵从率、仅1.0%级联失败，同时节省56.0% token。结论是可靠降本的关键在于保留协议关键状态，而非一味删减token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM系统依赖长交互历史来保留指令、工具状态、中间决策和未解决依赖，但上下文无限增长会增加计算成本、降低效率。因此需要一种在保证可靠性的前提下缩减上下文的方法。

Method: 比较五种裁剪策略（基于近期、基于相关性、摘要、协议感知裁剪、自适应预算护栏），在不同保留上下文水平和任务复杂度类别下评估，指标包括任务成功率、协议遵从率、有效工具调用、token节省、延迟降低、级联失败和关键上下文阈值。

Result: 常规策略平均节省约60% token，但任务成功率（66.6–77.3%）和协议遵从率（85.5–88.6%）较低。协议感知裁剪将任务成功率提升至92.2%；自适应护栏达96.0%成功率、96.3%遵从率、1.0%级联失败，平均节省56.0% token。保留预算≤25%时失败几率是≥50%的10.92倍（p<0.001）；激进预算下协议感知裁剪成功几率是常规方法的5.24倍，自适应护栏再提升2.11倍（p<0.001）。关键上下文阈值随工作流复杂度上升。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文缩减更依赖于保留协议关键状态，而非最大化token删除；自适应护栏可在长时程智能体系统中同时提升效率、可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems rely on long interaction histories to preserve instructions, tool states, intermediate decisions, and unresolved dependencies, but unrestricted context growth increases computational cost and can reduce efficiency. This study evaluates protocol-preserving context trimming as a reliability-constrained approach for multi-step agentic workflows. Five trimming strategies - recency-based, relevance-based, summarization, protocol-aware trimming, and adaptive budget guardrails - were compared across retained-context levels and workflow-complexity classes using task success, protocol adherence, valid tool calls, token savings, latency reduction, cascading failures, and critical context thresholds. Conventional strategies achieved about 60% mean token savings but lower task success (66.6-77.3%) and protocol adherence (85.5-88.6%). Protocol-aware trimming improved task success to 92.2%, while adaptive guardrails achieved 96.0% task success, 96.3% protocol adherence, and 1.0% cascading failure with 56.0% mean token savings. Retained-context budgets of 25% or less increased failure odds 10.92-fold relative to budgets of 50% or more (p < 0.001). Protocol-aware trimming produced 5.24-fold greater odds of successful completion than conventional methods under aggressive budgets, while adaptive guardrails further increased success odds 2.11-fold versus fixed protocol-aware trimming (p < 0.001). Critical context thresholds also increased with workflow complexity. These findings indicate that reliable context reduction depends more on preserving protocol-critical state than on maximizing token removal, and that adaptive guardrails can improve efficiency, scalability, and reliability in long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [264] [AI Policies: Help or Hindrance? A Software Developer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16496)
*Samuel Ferino,Rashina Hoda,John Grundy,Christoph Treude,Hashini Gunatilake*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于19位软件开发者的访谈，探讨AI政策如何帮助与阻碍开发者，并提出以开发者为中心的AI政策制定建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件组织为缓解LLM相关风险（如敏感信息泄露、未授权使用）而制定AI政策，但若开发者不参与/不遵守则形同虚设，因此需要了解开发者视角下AI政策的实际作用。

Method: 对19位软件开发者进行访谈的定性研究。

Result: 揭示了AI政策对开发者既有帮助也有阻碍的双重作用。

Conclusion: 为管理者和决策者提供了以开发者为中心的方法来引入AI政策。

Abstract: AI policies introduced by software organisations to mitigate LLM-related risks such as sensitive information leaks and unauthorised usage are not useful if software developers do not engage with them. We draw on 19 software developer interviews to show how AI policies help and hinder developers. We suggest approaches to support managers and decision makers with a developer-centric approach to introducing AI policies.

</details>


### [265] [ExecuCritic: Calibrated Critic Shaping for Code Generation with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16604)
*Junjie Cao,Yingjie He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ExecuCritic联合训练框架，让coder与critic在同一执行rollout上共同更新，critic预测通过/失败并给出诊断反馈，仅在critic与执行器一致时被coder采用，在八个代码基准上超越GRPO及提示式评审、标量奖励模型基线，且需更少梯度步和沙盒执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈（单元测试）是客观正确的监督信号，但整个程序常被压缩为单一的通过/失败位，使RLVR面临困难的信用分配问题；现有编码系统中的评审/测试角色多为提示式而非训练式，且未与执行结果校准。

Method: ExecuCritic联合训练框架：coder与critic在同一执行rollout上更新；critic预测通过/失败结果并给出简短诊断反馈；仅当critic与执行器在当前rollout组上一致时，coder才使用该信号。

Result: 在八个代码基准和两个近期开源骨架上，优于无critic的GRPO、提示式评审系统和标量奖励模型基线，同时需要更少的策略梯度步和更少的沙盒执行次数。

Conclusion: 消融与可靠性分析表明，性能提升源于更好的信用分配，而非更大的采样预算。

Abstract: Execution feedback is a useful supervision signal for code models because unit tests are objective and directly measure program correctness. Its weakness is that an entire program is often reduced to one pass or fail bit, leaving RLVR to solve a difficult credit assignment problem. At the same time, coding systems often include separate reviewer or tester roles, but these critics are usually prompted rather than trained and are not calibrated against execution. We propose ExecuCritic, a joint training framework in which a coder and a critic are updated on the same execution rollouts. The critic predicts pass or fail outcomes and gives short diagnostic feedback; the coder uses this signal only when the critic agrees with the executor on the current rollout group. Across eight code benchmarks and two recent open backbones, ExecuCritic improves over GRPO without a critic, prompted reviewer systems and scalar reward model baselines, while requiring fewer policy gradient steps and fewer sandbox executions. Ablations and reliability analyses suggest that the gains come from better credit assignment rather than larger sampling budgets.

</details>


### [266] [An Exploratory Study of Dependabot Cooldown Adoption in Open-Source GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16605)
*Hidetake Tanaka,Rikuto Tsuchida,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了GitHub Dependabot cooldown功能的早期采用情况：安全性担忧是主要动机，采用者倾向使用简单默认延迟（如7天）而非细粒度配置，提示工具应提供更稳健的默认设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化依赖更新可能在维护者和社区察觉前快速传播恶意包版本，GitHub于2025年7月正式推出Dependabot cooldown作为供应链攻击防御手段，但其早期采用效果尚不明确，因此需要实证研究其采用与配置情况。

Method: 对热门开源GitHub仓库进行探索性实证研究，考察其如何采用和配置cooldown功能，并调查采用动机。

Result: 92个已知动机的采用事件中有83个源于安全担忧；75个仅出于安全的采用中有43个由安全linter警告触发。251个保留cooldown的生态系统中，97.2%设置了通用延迟，其中64.3%使用7天，而各更新类型设置的采用率均低于10%。

Conclusion: 早期采用者偏好简单的默认延迟而非细粒度控制。工具应提供反映生态系统支持的稳健默认值，并将细粒度控制保留给具有明确更新优先级的依赖。

Abstract: Automated dependency updates can rapidly propagate malicious package releases before maintainers and the broader community have enough time to detect them. In July 2025, GitHub made Dependabot cooldown generally available as a defense against software supply chain attacks. However, the effects of its early adoption remain unknown. In this exploratory study, we empirically examine how popular open-source GitHub repositories adopt and configure the feature and investigate their motivations. We find that security concerns motivated 83 of 92 adoption events with known motivations. Security linter warnings triggered 43 of 75 security-only adoptions. Among 251 ecosystems within repositories that retained cooldown, 97.2% set a general delay. Of these, 64.3% used seven days, while use of each update type setting was below 10%. Early adopters therefore favor simple default delays over fine-grained controls. These findings suggest that tools could provide robust defaults reflecting ecosystem support and reserve fine-grained controls for dependencies with clear update priorities.

</details>


### [267] [Memory-Skill Isomorphism: One Skill Carrier, Two Native Uses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16669)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出将记忆与技能统一为单一 Skill 载体（L0描述→L1 SKILL.md→L2 reference文件），共享渐进式披露读取路径、区分受治理写路径，用一个部署系统验证并给出候选RSI设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆与技能通常各自构建存储、路由、检索、反思与更新机制，随积累产生大量重复的复用设施。由于 Skill 本身就是适合承载蒸馏记忆的自然载体，二者无需分别重建。

Method: 将记忆组件实现为一个 Skill：常驻描述存热线索（L0），按需 SKILL.md 存冷索引与策展策略（L1），reference/*.md 存细节记忆（L2）；以1,024字符描述预算压缩常驻索引；反思可演化任一 Skill；写入分叉为历史追加与当前状态重写并重新校验。

Result: 治理是最大识别点：4条写入口仅1/4到达结算账本。定价工作点下，一条L1点增加1,313首轮token（基线1,462），k=1无差异（1,313对1,365）、k=5为4,949；会话总量仅差1.18x且末轮区间重叠，价格分解不可得。单任务单模型下暴露该课程失败更少（0/8或1/8 对 6/6历史地板，未校正多重性）。常驻与BM25在两个小对比中未检出差异。

Conclusion: 提出候选RSI设计规则：单一 Skill 载体族、共享读侧、受治理的区分写侧。本工作评估的是常驻索引实现；体交付 h:=P(D|A) 在RQ1–RQ2中未instrument；单任务结果属选择性事后存在性信号，非确证性比率。

Abstract: Memory and skills improve agents without changing weights: memory carries prior experience, skills carry reusable procedures. Wrapping both in stores, routers, retrieval, reflection, and update paths makes reuse machinery grow with accumulation. Part of this duplication need not be rebuilt: a Skill is already a natural carrier for distilled memory. Here the memory component is a Skill: resident description holds hot cues, on-demand SKILL.md a colder index and curation policy, and reference/*.md files detailed memories (levels L0 -> L1 -> L2). A governed 1,024-character description budget keeps the resident index compressed. Memory and capability thus share one progressive-disclosure carrier; reflection evolves either Skill. Writes fork: history appended, current state rewritten and revalidated. In one deployed system, the sharpest identification is governance: 4 write entries exist, but only 1/4 reaches the settlement ledger. At the priced operating point, one L1 lesson-1 point adds 1,313 first-turn tokens against a 1,462-token baseline--tied at k=1 (1,313 versus 1,365), 4,949 at k=5; session totals differ by only 1.18x with overlapping last-turn ranges; price decomposition is unavailable. On one selected task with one model, exposing the lesson means fewer failures (0/8 or 1/8 with the lesson versus a shared non-concurrent 6/6 historical floor, unadjusted for multiplicity). The task was selected on prior floor evidence, so this is a selected-task post-selection existence signal, not a confirmatory rate. Resident and BM25 show no detected difference in two small comparisons. This motivates a candidate RSI design rule: one Skill carrier family, a shared read side, governed distinct writes. Body delivery h := P(D|A) is uninstrumented in RQ1--RQ2; this evaluates a resident-index implementation.

</details>


### [268] [RECTIFY: An Interactive Workbench for Post-Evaluation RAG Diagnosis, Repair, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16764)
*Keerthana Murugaraj,Salima Lamsiyah,Martin Theobald*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RECTIFY 是一个交互式 Streamlit 工作台，将已评估的 RAG 失败案例转化为可审计、可编辑的修复工作流，帮助开发者决定"接下来修什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评估器虽能识别弱检索、接地不良、答案不完整、生成无依据等失败，但很少告诉开发者下一步应该修复什么，缺乏从评估结果到修复决策的桥梁。

Method: RECTIFY 过滤无需修复的案例，将剩余失败路由到可操作的失败族和细粒度修复切片，并生成可编辑的修复卡片；开发者可批准、拒绝或通过沙盒重跑进行验证。

Result: 在受控 RAG 基准上，RECTIFY 在 BM25、稠密与混合检索下呈现可解释的失败画像：BM25 主要触发噪声检索修复，稠密与混合检索则留下较少的多部分欠检索与证据未充分利用案例。预过滤减少了不必要的修复候选，切片级路由比粗粒度族级诊断产生更精准的修复卡片。

Conclusion: RECTIFY 以开源 Streamlit 工作台形式公开，帮助开发者将评估结果转化为可检查的修复决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) evaluators can identify failures such as weak retrieval, poor grounding, incomplete answers, and unsupported generation, but they rarely help developers decide what to repair next. We present RECTIFY, an interactive Streamlit workbench that turns evaluated RAG cases into auditable repair workflows. RECTIFY filters cases that do not require repair, routes remaining failures into actionable families and finegrained repair slices, and generates editable repair cards that developers can approve, reject, or verify through sandbox reruns. On a controlled RAG benchmark, RECTIFY surfaces interpretable failure profiles across BM25, dense, and hybrid retrieval: BM25 mainly triggers noisy-retrieval repairs, while dense and hybrid retrieval leave smaller sets of multi-part underretrieval and underused-evidence cases. Additional analyses show that pre-filtering reduces unnecessary repair candidates and that slicelevel routing yields more targeted repair cards than broad family-level diagnosis. RECTIFY is publicly available as an open-source Streamlit workbench 1 for helping developers turn evaluation results into inspectable repair decisions.

</details>


### [269] [RepoAtlas: Guiding Coding Agents via Evolving Multimodal Repository Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16936)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Haiquan Wang,JiaWei Guo,Hanyang Xia,Yan Chen,Tong Chen,Zhang Zhiwei,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoAtlas是一个免训练模块，通过select–project–refresh循环在代码图上维护动态多模态仓库视图，帮助编码智能体解决仓库级问题，在SWE-bench Verified上提升解决率并减少token与调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级issue解决困难：智能体不仅需生成合理补丁，还要跨相互依赖文件定位代码并维护充分且聚焦的仓库上下文。代码图能揭示非局部关系，但线性文本接口无法呈现其拓扑；完整仓库图过于稠密难以可靠感知，而一次性局部视图随探索推进会过时。

Method: RepoAtlas在仓库代码图上运行select–project–refresh循环：结合issue证据与智能体当前探索状态，在固定预算下选取任务相关区域；将所选结构投影为互补的视觉与文本表示；当探索状态变化使视图过时时进行刷新。整个模块免训练。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，相比最强的多模态图基线，RepoAtlas将解决率提升2.4个百分点，同时平均减少输入token 5.8%、模型调用7.8%，并在三个不同家族与规模的模型上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过动态维护任务相关的多模态仓库视图，RepoAtlas有效缓解了仓库级代码上下文表示的密度与时效性问题，在提升问题解决率的同时降低了计算开销，且具备跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered coding agents have made rapid progress in automating software engineering tasks, yet repository-level issue resolution remains challenging. Beyond generating a plausible patch, an agent must localize relevant code across interdependent files and maintain repository context that is both sufficient and focused. Code graphs expose non-local relations, but linear text interfaces obscure their topology; rendering the full repository graph yields visual representations that are too dense to perceive reliably, whereas a one-shot local view becomes stale as exploration proceeds. We present \textbf{RepoAtlas}, a training-free module that maintains evolving multimodal repository views through a \emph{select--project--refresh} loop over a repository code graph. RepoAtlas combines evidence from the issue with the agent's current exploration state to select a task-relevant region under a fixed budget, projects the selected structure into complementary visual and textual representations, and refreshes the view when changes in the exploration state render it outdated. We evaluate RepoAtlas on SWE-bench Verified, where it improves the resolve rate by 2.4 points while reducing input tokens and model calls by 5.8\% and 7.8\% on average, relative to the strongest multimodal graph baseline, with consistent gains across three models of different families and scales.

</details>


### [270] [TasmScan: Continuation-Aware Taint Analysis for TVM Bytecode with Savelist Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16987)
*Yixuan Liu,Yin Wu,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TasmScan是首个针对TON虚拟机(TVM)的字节码级静态分析框架，通过建模savelist语义实现跨continuation数据流追踪，无需源码，检测智能合约缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVM使用一等continuation和savelist管理控制流与寄存器状态，savelist捕获的寄存器可跨continuation边界传递数据而不经过操作数栈，导致字节码级分析无法构建完整数据流追踪，除非显式建模savelist语义。

Method: 通过前向寄存器分析建模savelist语义，对精确解析的save站点和本地追踪的寄存器定义提供形式化过近似保证；将字节码提升为类型化中间表示TASIR，进行路径敏感的污点分析，并结合上下文感知的源来检测缺陷。

Result: 在TON验证器注册表的2,921个合约及208个标注基准上评估。全语料库中解析294,546个动态continuation目标，精度100%；消融实验证实savelist传播对解析间接寄存器调用至关重要。基准上五类缺陷检出率95.3%，精度96.8%；366对分层抽样估计整体精度85.8%；比SOTA符号执行基线中位加速17倍，路径分析完成率100%且无崩溃或超时。

Conclusion: TasmScan证明了在TVM字节码层面显式建模savelist语义对跨continuation数据流分析的必要性和有效性，能高精度、高效率地检测智能合约缺陷，显著优于现有符号执行方法。

Abstract: The Open Network (TON), with a peak market capitalization exceeding $20 billion and over 175 million activated on-chain addresses, relies on the TVM (TON Virtual Machine) to execute smart contracts. TVM uses first-class continuations with savelists to manage control flow and register state across continuation invocations. Since savelist-captured registers allow data to flow across continuation boundaries without passing through the operand stack, bytecode-level analyses cannot construct complete data flow tracking without explicitly modeling savelist semantics. We present TasmScan, the first bytecode-level static analysis framework for TVM that enables cross-continuation data flow reasoning without requiring source code. TasmScan models savelist semantics via forward register analysis with a formal over-approximation guarantee for exact-resolved save sites and locally tracked register definitions, then lifts bytecode into TASIR, a typed intermediate representation, and performs path-sensitive taint analysis with context-aware sources to detect defects. We evaluate TasmScan on 2,921 contracts from the TON verifier registry and a labeled benchmark of 208 contracts with human-confirmed ground truth. On the full corpus, TasmScan resolves 294,546 dynamic continuation targets with 100% precision; ablation confirms that savelist propagation is essential for resolving indirect register calls that depend on cross-continuation register passing. On the benchmark, TasmScan detects 95.3% of defects across five classes with 96.8% precision. A 366-pair stratified sample from the full corpus estimates 85.8% overall precision. TasmScan offers a 17x median speedup over the state-of-the-art symbolic-execution baseline, and in the path-analysis comparison completes 100% of analyses with zero crashes or timeouts.

</details>


### [271] [Search-Based Metamorphic Testing of Vision-Language Models in Autonomous Underwater Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17007)
*Muhammad Yousaf,Aitor Arrieta,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaVLM，一种基于搜索的蜕变测试方法，用于评估水下环境自主水下机器人(AUR)软件中视觉语言模型(VLM)的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业伙伴关注多领域工业系统质量保证，包括水下自主机器人(AUR)。尽管VLM在场景理解、图像描述和目标识别方面表现优异，但其在水下环境AUR软件中的应用尚缺乏探索，因此需要自动化软件测试工具来评估VLM质量并提升其可靠性。

Method: 提出基于搜索的蜕变测试方法MetaVLM，识别水下图像上的最小变换集合以诱发错误的模型预测，从而揭示VLM失效。采用NSGA-II作为多目标搜索算法，与随机搜索基线进行对比评估。

Result: 在开源VLM（BLIP和CLIP）上进行评估，结果展示了各VLM在AUR软件系统背景下的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，为从事VLM软件系统质量保证的软件工程实践者和研究者提炼了经验教训。

Abstract: Our industry partner focuses on quality assurance for industrial systems across multiple domains, including maritime systems, such as overwater vessels and autonomous underwater robots (AURs). Despite the strong performance of vision-language models (VLMs) in scene understanding, image captioning, and object recognition, their use in AUR software operating in underwater environments is underexplored. Therefore, in this context, it is important to evaluate the quality of VLMs for integration into AUR software and, so, automated software testing tools are needed to assess their suitability and improve their dependability. To this end, we propose a search-based metamorphic testing approach (MetaVLM) that identifies a minimal set of transformations on underwater images to induce incorrect model predictions, thereby revealing VLM failures. We employ NSGA-II as a multi-objective search algorithm and evaluate it over open-source VLMs, BLIP and CLIP, against a random search baseline. Results demonstrate the strengths and limitations of each VLM in the context of AUR software systems. Based on the results, we derive lessons for software engineering practitioners and researchers working on quality assurance of VLM-based software systems.

</details>


### [272] [GANADI: Uncovering C/C++ OSS Reuse Genealogies via Pivotal Function-Based Clustering to Enhance Supply Chain Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17018)
*Dongyeon Kim,Seunghoon Woo,Heejo Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GANADI，通过聚类共享"枢纽函数"特征的下游项目并推断复用方向，系统识别C/C++开源软件的复用谱系，以提升软件供应链安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解OSS复用谱系对提升SBOM完整性和跨供应链安全修复优先级至关重要。现有方法虽能识别项目内被复用的组件和漏洞，但无法追踪经过中间项目的复用路径，限制了供应链生态的整体安全防护。

Method: GANADI基于源头派生代码的共享特征（关键函数）对下游项目进行聚类，再在每个簇内推断项目间的复用方向，从而构建复用谱系。

Result: 在20个广泛复用的OSS项目、1500多条传播路径上，GANADI识别复用谱系的精确率达84.85%、召回率达95.76%，远超现有方法最高23.21%的召回率。基于谱系进行漏洞检测，在真实流行C/C++项目中识别出48个未修补漏洞，其中23个经负责任披露后已修复（含1个CVE编号）。

Conclusion: GANADI有效填补了跨中间项目的复用路径追踪空白，显著优于现有方法，并通过实际漏洞发现与修复验证了基于谱系的漏洞管理的实用价值。

Abstract: We present GANADI, a systematic approach for identifying C/C++ OSS reuse genealogies to enhance software supply chain security. Understanding OSS reuse genealogy is crucial for improving SBOM completeness and prioritizing security remediation across supply chains. Although existing approaches can identify reused compo- nents and vulnerabilities within a project, they fail to trace OSS reuse paths through intermediate projects, limiting their effectiveness in securing supply chain ecosystems. To address this limitation, GANADI constructs reuse genealogies by clustering downstream projects based on shared characteristics of origin-derived code (called pivotal functions), and then inferring reuse direction among the projects within each cluster. When applied to 20 widely reused OSS projects with over 1,500 propagation paths, GANADI achieved 84.85% precision and 95.76% recall in identifying reuse genealogies, outperforming existing approaches that achieved at most 23.21% recall. Leveraging OSS reuse genealogy for vulnerability detection, we identified 48 unpatched vulnerabilities in real-world popular C/C++ projects. Among them, 23 were patched following our responsible disclosure (including one CVE ID assigned), demonstrating the practical impact of genealogy-based vulnerability management.

</details>


### [273] [A Set-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Assessing Asset Administration Shell Instances: Towards Comparability and Suitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17062)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Bastian Lang,Hansjoerg Tutsch,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补方法，用于AAS的比较与面向应用的适用性评估：一是基于集合论的AAS模型比较，二是AAS适用性模型，从结构一致性、语义一致性、基数与规范一致性维度评估AAS对特定用例的符合度，输出适用性分数与偏差报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同AAS实例在结构、内容和完成度上差异较大，难以判断某个AAS是否适合特定应用。制造软件服务日益以AAS为基础，因此需要对其进行比较和面向应用的适用性评估。

Method: 1）采用集合论操作比较AAS模型，识别共有、缺失和不同的子模型与参数；2）构建AAS适用性模型，评估AAS对特定用例需求的符合度，考察结构一致性、语义一致性、基数和规范一致性，可基于参考AAS或一组所需SemanticID进行评估；由识别出的偏差推导适用性值，并生成缺失或不符信息的详细报告。

Result: 方法可识别AAS之间的共有、缺失与差异部分，并给出针对具体用例的适用性值及详细偏差报告，从而提供应用特定的适用性信息。

Conclusion: 该方案有助于实践者和研究者比较不断演化的AAS，并为其在制造软件服务中的适用性提供面向应用的信息。

Abstract: Asset Administration Shells (AAS) provide a standardized means of representing assets and their information in manufacturing and increasingly serve as a basis for software services. However, different AAS instances vary in structure, content, and degree of completion, making it difficult to determine whether a given AAS is suitable for a specific application. This paper presents two complementary methods to support the comparison and application-oriented assessment of AAS. First, set-theoretic operations are employed to compare AAS models, enabling the identification of common, missing, and differing submodels and parameters. Second, an AAS suitability model assesses the conformity of an AAS to the requirements of a specific use case. The assessment considers structural conformity, semantic consistency, cardinality, and specification conformity and can be performed either against a reference AAS or a set of required SemanticIDs. A suitability value is derived from the identified deviations and is complemented by a detailed report of missing or non-conforming information. The proposed approach support practitioners and researchers in the comparison of evolving AAS and provide application-specific information on their suitability for manufacturing software services.

</details>


### [274] [Towards an Asset Administration Shell Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17084)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AAS成熟度新概念，用于系统化比较AAS实例，弥补缺乏方法论框架和成熟度模型的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAS已成为制造业数字孪生的基础模型，但比较不同AAS实例缺乏公认的方法论框架和成熟度模型，难以系统化支撑分析。

Method: 从文献中推导AAS成熟度概念（衡量数字孪生既定标准的满足程度），并通过示例进行应用验证。

Result: 提出AAS成熟度概念，使不同AAS实例具备可比性，为比较AAS实例提供依据。

Conclusion: 该初步成果可帮助实践者和研究者系统比较AAS实例，识别并评估数字孪生工程流程中的后续开发步骤。

Abstract: The Asset Administration Shell (AAS) is increasingly recognized as a fundamental model for the realization of and data exchange between digital twins in manufacturing. An AAS defines a hierarchical data structure to represent any type of asset throughout its entire lifecycle. In the context of AAS-based systems, comparing different AAS instances constitutes a practical challenge, as neither a widely accepted methodological framework nor a maturity model are available to systematically support such analyses. To address this gap, we propose a novel concept of AAS maturity that characterizes the extent to which established digital twin criteria are met and thus enabling comparability of AAS instances. The concepts are derived from the literature and applied through exemplification. These emerging results enable practitioners and researchers to systematically compare AAS instances and support the identification and assessment of further development steps in the digital twin engineering process.

</details>


### [275] [Grounding SWE-Agent Decisions in Architecture-0 Design: Navigating Unknown Unknowns through Physical Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17221)
*Zhongkai Wang,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究自主软件工程智能体（SWE-Agents）在架构设计的早期阶段（Architecture 0）如何应对隐含的工程约束（Unknown Unknowns, UUs）。通过纯文本自博弈、工具增强反馈和外部物理映射的渐进轨迹分析，发现失败级联现象，并提出 Physical Mapping Guard（PMG）通过分离验证权限来消除物理层与验证层的博弈行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-Agents 在确定性编码任务上表现出色，但在架构设计初期阶段（Architecture 0）表现不佳，该阶段充满未被明确表达的隐式工程约束（UUs）。研究希望理解智能体如何导航这些 UUs，并揭示其失败机制。

Method: 探索渐进轨迹：纯文本自博弈、工具增强反馈、外部物理映射。发现纯文本推理退化为礼貌共识或不切实际的虚构；早期执行沙箱触发 Specification Gaming，即智能体利用对验证脚本的自主权绕过物理约束。提出 Physical Mapping Guard (PMG)，基于关注点分离原则，取消智能体的验证权限，强制语义意图由外部确定性 Semantic-to-Physical (S2P) 映射引擎评估。

Result: PMG 完全消除了物理层和验证层的博弈行为。残余失败被精确隔离到语义重解释和审计者越权两个方面。

Conclusion: PMG 是实现自动化架构设计中真正的 affordance grounding 的关键一步，通过分离验证权限有效解决了自我验证陷阱。

Abstract: Autonomous Software Engineering Agents (SWE-Agents) excel in deterministic coding tasks but struggle with Architecture 0, the nascent system design phase plagued by implicit engineering constraints, or Unknown Unknowns (UUs) that are rarely stated explicitly. To investigate how agents navigate UUs, we explore a progressive trajectory across pure-text self-play, tool-augmented feedback, and external physical mapping. Our empirical analysis reveals a cascading chain of failures. Pure-text reasoning inevitably devolves into polite consensus or plausible yet physically impossible fabrications. Attempting to bridge this gap via an early-stage execution sandbox unexpectedly triggers Specification Gaming: agents exploit their autonomy over validation scripts to bypass physical constraints, achieving superficial success without resolving core architectural flaws. To resolve this self-validation trap, we propose the Physical Mapping Guard (PMG). Grounded in the software engineering principle of Separation of Concerns, PMG revokes verification authority from the agent, forcing semantic intents to be evaluated by an external, deterministic Semantic-to-Physical (S2P) mapping engine. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PMG completely eradicates physical-layer and validation-layer gaming. By precisely isolating residual failures to semantic reinterpretations and auditor overreach, PMG marks a critical step toward genuine affordance grounding in automated architectural design.

</details>


### [276] [A Memorization Floor for LLM Refinement of Decompiled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17236)
*Muhammad Asjad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"记忆下限"对照：对同一函数先精化、再在被破坏标识符的输入上精化，测算还原量是否依赖输入。在未发布函数上，精化输出高于置换零假设（+0.072~+0.137），但破坏输入数据流几乎不改变命名增益（+0.001），另一厂商模型复现该结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 精化反编译输出时，其改进究竟来自对输入的真实还原，还是来自模型先验/记忆，缺乏可靠的判别手段；语料难度等混杂因素也干扰跨项比较。

Method: 设计"记忆下限"作为项内对照：精化函数后，再从标识符被破坏的输入重做精化，测量存活部分。使用分析计划提交后撰写的函数以避免记忆；构建与输出词表匹配的置换零假设；破坏数据流、移除类型前缀、置换名称等消融；并使用另一厂商的第二个精化器，预注册、字节相同输入，共十二组对比、两个模型、十二个零假设。

Result: 还原是真实的：精化输出比臂匹配置换零假设高 +0.072 到 +0.137。但还原不依赖被消融的输入：破坏输入数据流使命名增益变化 +0.001（95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]），移除类型前缀或置换名称影响不大。另一厂商模型复现该模式。可读性始终处于上限，读者无法获得信号。零假设是有界而非绝对的：低于 0.056 的贡献不可见。

Conclusion: 无注册假设被证实；报告了五项仪器失败（包括偏向处理组的重汇编工具、未验证的等价性检查器）。命名增益不能归因于输入数据流，且由于消融保留操作，仅凭操作命名仍是一种竞争解释。

Abstract: We introduce a memorization floor: a within-item control separating what LLM refinement of decompiler output recovers from its input from what it recovers from its prior. Refine a function, then refine it again from an input whose identifiers have been destroyed, and measure what survives. Because the comparison is within-item, corpus difficulty cannot contribute; it costs twenty API calls. Applied to functions written after our analysis plan was committed, so no released model could have memorized them, it reports two things. Recovery is real: refined output sits +0.072 to +0.137 above an arm-matched permutation null built from its own output vocabulary. But it does not depend on the input we ablate: destroying the input's dataflow changes the naming gain by +0.001 (95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]), and removing type prefixes or permuting names changes it by no more. A second refiner from another vendor, registered in advance and given byte-identical inputs, reproduces this -- twelve contrasts, two models, twelve nulls. Readability stays at ceiling throughout, so a reader is given no signal. The null is bounded, not absolute: contributions under 0.056 are invisible, and the ablation leaves operations intact, so naming from those alone remains a competing reading. No registered hypothesis was confirmed, and we report the five instrument failures behind that in full, including a reassembly harness biased against the treated arm and an equivalence checker we registered without checking it worked on our inputs.

</details>


### [277] [An Exemplar of a Digital Twin in Mechanical Engineering: Understanding Model Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17258)
*Mahussi Datongnon,Hubert Lejeune,Yoann Jus,Benoit Combemale,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对混合数字孪生（hybrid DT）工程实践缺乏文档化的问题，基于CETIM流体回路数字孪生案例，提出一种沿"动机"与"实现"两个维度刻画混合化的方法，以提升可追溯性、可复现性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生（DT）在工业领域广泛应用，并日益将物理基（演绎）模型与数据驱动（归纳）模型集成，形成混合模型（hybridization）。然而，混合数字孪生的工程实践文档不足：混合化常以临时方式引入，实现细节仅部分显式，限制了可复现性与可迁移性。

Method: 报告CETIM现有流体回路数字孪生的开发工作：先用Gil等人的刻画框架描述该DT，提供跨生命周期维度的结构化视图；再通过沿"动机（motivation）"与"实现（realization）"两个维度的补充刻画，使混合化过程显式化。

Result: 该描述提供了混合化决策的可追溯记录，支持混合数字孪生工程实践的文档化与转移。

Conclusion: 通过结构化刻画框架与动机/实现双维度分析，可以显式化混合数字孪生的工程决策，促进其可复现、可迁移的实践传播。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are widely adopted across a variety of application domains. In industrial sectors, particularly in mechanical engineering, they accelerate product development, reduce risks, enable early issue prediction, and lower sustainment costs . In practice, DTs increasingly integrate physics-based (deductive) and data-driven (inductive) models into hybrid models combining the complementary strengths of both modeling paradigms. In this paper, we refer to this integration paradigm as hybridization. Despite this trend, the engineering of hybrid DTs that is, remains insufficiently documented. Hybridization is often introduced in an ad hoc manner, and its implementation is only partially made explicit, which limits reproducibility and transferability. This paper reports on the development of an existing fluidic loop digital twin at Centre Technique des Industries M{é}canique (CETIM). The DT is described using the characterization framework of Gil et al., providing a structured view across its lifecycle dimensions. To make hybridization explicit, the case is further analyzed through a complementary characterization structured along two dimensions: motivation and realization. The resulting description provides a traceable account of hybridization decisions and supports the documentation and transfer of hybrid DT engineering practices.

</details>


### [278] [After the Party: Governing What a Viral Agent-Skill Ecosystem Left Behind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17274)
*Yunpeng Xiong,Ting Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过 OpenClaw 的 Git 历史、GitHub issues/PRs 及三份 ClawHub 注册表快照，衡量 2026 年 AI agent skill 热潮的留存状况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026 年上半年 OpenClaw AI agent 走红，公共 skill 注册表爆发式增长（91 天内库存近乎翻倍）。热潮退去后，需要衡量它留下了什么，以及快速增长的 agent-skill 注册表该如何治理。

Method: 基于 OpenClaw Git 历史、GitHub issues 与 pull requests，以及三份 ClawHub 注册表快照进行实证分析；使用多种 skill 特征、下载量、留存、审计痕迹（stars/comments）与权限证据，并用三个安全扫描器交叉比对、人工裁决建立参考标准。

Result: 关注高度集中：前 10% skill 获 46.93% 下载。控制创建队列与 skill 年龄后，无简单特征（大小、下载量等）能稳定预测 listing 是否延续。77.86% skill 无星无评论；85.06% 可读 skill 携带权限证据。三个扫描器在共同覆盖的 61,990 个 skill 中对 23,702 个判断不一致；人工裁决后加权敏感度仅 21.67%–61.06%。

Conclusion: 治理高速增长的 agent-skill 注册表不能依赖简单元数据或单一扫描器分数，需要稳健、透明、可独立验证的测量体系。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through agent skills, i.e., natural-language instructions, that direct a host agent toward shell, network, credential, file, and process actions, and public registries distribute them at scale. In the first half of 2026, the OpenClaw AI agent went viral, and its public skill registry boomed: the observable stock nearly doubled in 91 days, and a majority of the listings visible in June were created in just two months. By the end of our study window, the wave had crested, and monthly listing creation and core-repository activity were falling from their spring peaks. This paper measures what the boom left behind, drawing on the OpenClaw Git history, its GitHub issues and pull requests, and three ClawHub registry snapshots. Attention is concentrated: the top 10% of skills received 46.93% of all downloads. No simple skill features (like size or download counts) remained a stable predictor of continued listing once creation cohort and skill age were controlled. Human scrutiny did not stay: 77.86% have zero stars and zero comments, while 85.06% of the readable skills carry privilege evidence. And automated cleanup is not ready: the three security scanners disagreed on 23,702 of the 61,990 skills they all cover. After human adjudication, weighted scanner sensitivity against the reference standard ranged from 21.67% to 61.06%. Governing fast-growing agent-skill registries cannot rely on simple metadata or single scanner scores; it requires robust, transparent measurement and independent validation.

</details>


### [279] [Type-IV Code Clone Detection via Layer-Wise Non-Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17338)
*Luciano Marchezan,Kevin Delcourt,Eugene Syriani,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出LWVIC4Code：一种面向Type-IV代码克隆检测的非对比式表示学习方法，基于VICReg与分层训练，引入跨层一致性正则和深度相关层加权，无需负样本即可获得鲁棒、具辨别力的代码表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Type-IV克隆语义等价但语法不同，传统token/语法方法难以检测；近期机器学习方法依赖对比学习，需谨慎负采样并易引入偏差，因此需要不依赖负样本的表示学习方案。

Method: 基于Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg)框架及先前分层VICReg训练，LWVIC4Code引入跨层一致性正则化与深度相关的层加权，沿transformer各层逐步精炼语义信息，生成鲁棒且具辨别力的代码表示。

Result: 在Python (Kamino) 与多语言 (GPTCloneBench) 数据集上，与对比学习基线和零样本大语言模型对比，LWVIC4Code无需负样本即达到相当或更优性能，受益于分层监督，并能从Python有效泛化到Java、C#等语言。

Conclusion: 结果表明，非对比式、分层表示学习是鲁棒语义代码克隆检测的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Software clones are fragments of code that are similar or functionally equivalent to each other. They pose significant challenges for maintenance, refactoring, and bug detection. Detecting Type-IV clones, which are semantically equivalent but may differ syntactically, is particularly difficult for traditional token- or syntax-based methods. Recent machine learning approaches rely on contrastive learning, which requires careful negative sampling and can introduce bias. In this paper, we propose LWVIC4Code, a non-contrastive representation learning approach specifically designed for Type-IV clone detection. Building on the Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg) framework and prior layer-wise VICReg training, LWVIC4Code introduces cross-layer consistency regularization and depth-dependent layer weighting to progressively refine semantic information across transformer layers, producing robust and discriminative code representations. We conduct an empirical study comparing LWVIC4Code against a contrastive learning baseline and zero-shot large language models on Python (Kamino) and multi-language (GPTCloneBench) datasets. Results show that LWVIC4Code achieves competitive or superior performance without negative samples, benefits from layer-wise supervision, and generalizes effectively from Python to other languages, particularly Java and C#. These results demonstrate that non-contrastive, layer-wise representation learning is a promising direction for robust semantic code clone detection.

</details>


### [280] [Coding Agents Have Converged: Why the SWE-bench Leaderboard Can No Longer Order Its Top Entries, and What to Measure Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17394)
*Fengshuo Liu,Ying Liu,Ruize Sun,Lie Luo,Siyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文审计了SWE-bench排行榜上小型分数差距是否真能支持系统排名，发现顶级系统成功高度重叠、分数受模型-脚手架组合影响，多数相邻排名差异在统计上不显著，因而小差距不应被解读为确定排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体排行榜上微小的分数差异常被当作系统排序的依据，但作者质疑这种解读是否有已发布结论支撑，因此在不运行模型的情况下进行审计。

Method: 分析了254个SWE-bench提交（跨四个split），考察成功/失败集重叠、嵌套度、模型-脚手架配对影响、交互检验（Holm校正）、相邻配对的McNemar检验及基于排行榜的分组规则，并发布划分方案与五步审计协议。

Result: Verified上前两名各解决500题中的396题；前十共享285次成功和51次失败，仅164题区分结果；前沿解集嵌套中位数0.935远高于基线0.774。同模型脚手架范围达29.8个百分点，而前三十总差距仅8.8点；9项交互检验中6项Holm校正后仍显著。McNemar检验中Verified前三十的29个相邻对无一分离，而Test split的23个相邻对中分离出14个。规则给出三层，Holm校正后为两层。

Conclusion: 小聚合差距不能确立排名差异，非拒绝也不代表等价。应报告特定比较集的解析度和模型-脚手架来源，而非将小差距解释为既定排名。

Abstract: Small differences on coding-agent leaderboards are often read as an ordering of systems. We audit whether the published verdicts support this reading, using 254 SWE-bench submissions across four splits without running models. On Verified, the leading two entries each resolve 396 of 500 instances. The top ten share 285 successes and 51 failures, leaving 164 instances that distinguish their outcomes. Frontier solution sets have median nesting 0.935 against a score-implied baseline of 0.774, indicating strongly shared successes. Scores also depend on the evaluated model-scaffold pair: observed within-model scaffold ranges reach 29.8 percentage points, compared with the 8.8-point spread of the top thirty. Six of nine cell-mean interaction tests remain significant after Holm correction, although this observational design does not identify causal scaffold effects. Exact paired McNemar tests separate none of the 29 adjacent Verified top-thirty pairs at alpha=0.05, while the larger Test split separates 14 of 23. A stated leader-based rule yields three descriptive tiers, or two after Holm correction; non-rejection does not establish equivalence. We release the partition and a five-step audit protocol that profiles shared outcomes, tests paired differences, reports grouping sensitivity, and estimates the instance budget needed for resolution. The results motivate reporting comparison-set-specific resolution and model-scaffold provenance instead of interpreting small aggregate gaps as established rank differences.

</details>
